7	> **Honesty note:** every engine-fact claim is tagged `[verified]` (read against engine code this session), `[design]` (proposed here), or `[researched]` (needs sign-off). Not legal/financial advice.
8	
9	---
10	
11	## 0. TL;DR
12	Build the **pick-rallies → reel** surface as a **hybrid**: a selectable rally **list/grid** is the workhorse; a **deterministic query bar** ("rallies over 10s, longest first") pre-selects into the **same** selection set; a sticky footer compiles the selection into one downloadable MP4. The entire MVP runs on **existing engine endpoints** — `POST /api/query` → `POST /api/compile` → `GET /api/highlights` `[verified backend/main.py:331-340, 342-396, 307-328]` — with **zero new engine intelligence and zero new endpoints**. The single most important caveat: queries and chips are scoped to **length / count / order / shot-count only**; shot-type, player, and score are **roadmap-only** (no detector, no column) and must not be faked. The biggest UX-vs-effort tension: **no per-rally preview is possible today** — only the *final compiled reel* can stream — so MVP selects on text metadata, and visual preview/timeline is a deliberate later milestone.
13	
14	## 1. Problem & goal
15	Today the alpha can detect rallies and stitch a reel, but reel-building is either a **silent `stitching.min_shot_count` threshold** `[verified backend/main.py:362-383]` or a static checkbox list whose data plumbing is even broken — the bundled static UI reads `data.rallies` while the API returns `play_segments`, so it renders nothing `[verified backend/api/static/app.js:344 vs backend/main.py:340]`. The owner's framing is **dynamic per-video selection, not a fixed threshold**: a coach should say "give me the long rallies from this session" and get exactly those, then fine-tune by hand.
16	
17	**The concrete outcome ("good"):** A coach uploads a 40-minute session; the engine finds 60 rallies. They type *"top 15 longest"*, eyeball the auto-selected cards, untick two warm-up rallies, name it *"Tuesday — long points"*, and download a ~6-minute reel — without learning any threshold knob.
18	
19	**Goal:** the smallest *honest* surface that turns engine-detected rallies into a user-curated, downloadable reel, with both **fast** (query) and **precise** (checkbox) selection sharing **one** state.
20	
21	**Non-goals (alpha):** no conversational LLM (`UI_PROPOSAL.md:66` defers it); no shot-type/player/score filters (no data exists); no per-rally video preview while it requires unbuilt streaming; no multi-tenant auth (single-box alpha).
22	
23	## 2. Decisions locked
24	| # | Decision | Source / date | Implication |
25	|---|----------|---------------|-------------|
26	| D1 | Dominant surface = selectable rally **list/grid** | Synthesis 2026-06-20 (Grid scored 15, fit-to-data 4) | Scales to long matches via bulk+filter; shows honest fields only |
27	| D2 | Fast entry = **deterministic** client-side `parseQuery()` (no LLM) | `[verified UI_PROPOSAL.md:66]` conversational QA deferred | Predictable, unit-testable, offline, zero API cost |
28	| D3 | **One** shared `Set<segment_id>`, mutated by query + checkbox + (future) timeline | Synthesis 2026-06-20 | No second compile path can diverge from what the user sees |
29	| D4 | Compile via existing `POST /api/compile {video_id, segment_ids, output_filename}` | `[verified backend/main.py:342-396]` | Selection order is harmless — stitcher `_merge_overlapping` de-dupes `[verified compiler.py:80-105, 311-317]` |
30	| D5 | Query grammar (alpha) = length, count, order, **shot_count only** | `[verified database.py:122-135, gemini.py:470-485]` | Only fields the payload actually returns; grey out the rest |
31	| D6 | MVP backend = **zero new endpoints** | `[verified]` reuse map (§4) | Ships the full loop on the already-shipped contract |
32	| D7 | Replace the silent `min_shot_count` cut with **explicit user selection** | Owner framing + `[verified main.py:362-383]` | UI *warns* about the floor; it never hides why a rally vanished |
33	| D8 | **Default selection = ON** (curate-by-removal) | Owner 2026-06-20 | Rallies load all-selected (matches today's "all rallies" reel); user removes warm-ups. Footer/empty UX assumes a non-empty start |
34	| D9 | **Preview-before-select is NOT a ship-blocker** for the MVP | Owner 2026-06-20 | MVP selects on text metadata + previews the *final compiled reel*; the source/clip-stream endpoint (per-rally preview) is the next milestone (P9), not MVP |
35	| D10 | **MVP is job-tethered** (carry `result.video_id`); no `GET /api/videos` | Owner 2026-06-20 | Smallest honest slice; multi-video home + identity scoping is P8 (gated) |
36	| D11 | `min_shot_count`: **warn, do NOT override** in the MVP | Owner 2026-06-20 | The UI surfaces the floor; an explicit-selection override of the compile default is deferred (would trigger the Launch Report convention) |
37	
38	## 3. Foundation — what the engine gives us today  `[verified this session]`
39	The whole design rests on the engine's real, already-shipped contract. Read against the engine repo (`badminton-highlight-indexer`):
40	
41	- **The rally store is `play_segments`** — columns `id, video_id, start_time, end_time, duration, server, receiver, metadata` `[verified database.py:124-135]`. `id` is the integer the UI selects and passes to compile; `start_time/end_time/duration` are REAL seconds (duration computed at insert).
42	- **Listing rallies = `POST /api/query {video_id}` → `{play_segments:[...]}`** — the *only* rally-list path today; it JSON-parses `metadata` and joins `events` `[verified main.py:331-340, database.py:427-475]`.
43	- **Building a reel = `POST /api/compile {video_id, segment_ids:[int], output_filename}`** — loads `videos.filepath`, filters all segments to the requested `segment_ids`, applies the `min_shot_count` floor, extracts `time_pairs=[(start,end)]`, and calls `VideoCompiler.compile_highlights(raw_path, segments, name)` `[verified main.py:342-396, compiler.py:303]`, which **merges overlaps** then per-clip re-encodes → concat → one MP4 `[verified compiler.py:311-317]`. Selection order/duplicates are therefore safe.
44	- **Downloading = `GET /api/highlights/{video_id}/{filename}`** — streams the compiled MP4. Compile returns a **server-local filepath, NOT a URL** `[verified main.py:394]`, so the SPA constructs this download URL itself. The compiled reel is the **only streamable video that exists today** `[verified main.py:307-328]`.
45	- **The floor knob is readable** — `GET /api/config` exposes `stitching.min_shot_count` `[verified main.py:121-123]`, so the UI can warn before a selected rally is silently dropped.
46	- **Job entry** — `GET /api/jobs/{job_id}` returns `result.video_id` `[verified main.py:274-293]`, which is how the MVP reaches a video without a (nonexistent) list-videos endpoint.
47	
48	**Honest fields vs fabricated/roadmap (critical for the UI):**
49	- **Honest today** (default `default_segmenter: "gemini"`, per `config.json` `[verified]`): `start_time`, `end_time`, `duration`, and `metadata.shot_count` (Gemini's `shuttle_exchange_count`, fallback `max(3, int(dur/0.8))`) + `metadata.shot_count_confidence` (a **string**, often `"Unknown"`) `[verified gemini.py:470-485]`. On the gemini path `server`/`receiver` are written `None` ("no fabricated data") `[verified gemini.py:480-487]`.
50	- **Fabricated — do NOT surface:** the `motion` demo segmenter fills `server`/`receiver` via `random.sample(...)`, a random `shot_count`, and random `events` (serve/smash/foul/winner) `[verified motion.py:191-198]`. `query` returns these columns + `events`, but they are not ground truth and must stay hidden.
51	- **Roadmap — no column, no detector:** shot-type per shot, player identity, match/point score, game/set grouping. None exist anywhere in the schema; do not consume them `[verified database.py whole-schema 122-148]`.
52	- **Correction-loop columns exist but are dead:** `candidate_segments.human_label/notes/confirmed_segment_id` are defined but no endpoint writes them `[verified database.py:166-168]` — that's the M4 corpus extension (engine PR-4/B6, `UI_ALPHA_EXECUTION_PLAN.md`).
53	
54	## 4. Design / architecture
55	
56	**Reuse map (existing — `[verified]`, no change for MVP):** `POST /api/query` (list) → `POST /api/compile` (stitch) → `GET /api/highlights/{video_id}/{filename}` (download); `GET /api/config` (floor) ; `GET /api/jobs/{id}` (carry `result.video_id`). **All net-new code is the SPA** in `khelsutra` `web/` (Vite + React + TS + Tailwind, per `web/README.md`), plus a small set of *optional* real-gap engine enablers (§4.3) — none required for MVP.
57	
58	**4.1 Components (net-new SPA)**
59	- **QueryBar + `parseQuery()`** — a *pure, deterministic, unit-tested* string → `{filter, sort}` compiler over `duration` / `ordinal` / `shot_count`. Renders a **parsed-pill** ("filter: duration > 10s · sort: duration desc") so the interpretation is transparent and hand-correctable; greys out shot-type/player/score tokens with an inline "not available yet". No LLM, no network call.
60	- **RallyList/Grid** — one card per `play_segment`: ordinal (by `start_time`), `mm:ss` start, duration (`end−start`), and a shot-count + confidence chip **only when `metadata.shot_count` is present** (absent on detector paths that don't write it). Checkbox per card; the thumbnail area is a placeholder (no endpoint yet).
61	- **`useRallySelection()` hook** — owns `selectedIds:Set<number>`, `displayOrder`, `apply(queryResult)`, `toggle(id)`, and bulk Select-all / Clear / Invert. Unit-tested (matches the exec-plan's "API client + review-state logic" bar).
62	- **SelectionFooter (sticky)** — live "N clips / total M:SS"; a **`min_shot_count` warning** when a selected rally would be dropped at compile (read from `GET /api/config`); reel-name input; one **Build** button.
63	- **CompileResult** — `<video src=GET /api/highlights/...>` preview of the *compiled reel* + a download link.
64	- **TypedApiClient** — `query`, `compile`, `buildDownloadUrl`; the typed seam to the engine.
65	
66	**4.2 Data flow** `[verified anchors inline]`
67	Gemini segmenter (process time) writes `play_segments {id, start/end_time, duration, metadata.shot_count/confidence/detector}` `[gemini.py:461-498]`
68	→ SPA `POST /api/query {video_id}` → `{play_segments:[...]}` `[main.py:331-340]` (**fix** the static UI's `data.rallies` read → `play_segments` `[app.js:344]`)
69	→ normalize to `RallyCard[]` + one shared `selectedIds:Set`; `server`/`receiver`/`events` are present in the payload but motion-only fabrications → **not surfaced** `[motion.py:191-198]`
70	→ all input modes mutate the **same** `selectedIds`: (a) `parseQuery()` predicate over duration/ordinal/shot_count; (b) checkbox toggle + bulk ops; (c) *(post-MVP)* timeline band
71	→ footer recomputes count + Σduration **client-side**, and warns for any selected rally with `shot_count < stitching.min_shot_count` `[main.py:362-383]`
72	→ **Build** → `POST /api/compile {video_id, segment_ids: [...selectedIds], output_filename}` → stitcher de-dupes via `_merge_overlapping` `[compiler.py:311-317]`, cuts `time_pairs` from `videos.filepath`, re-encodes per clip → concat → one MP4 `[main.py:386-393, compiler.py:303]`
73	→ compile returns a **server-local filepath, not a URL** `[main.py:394]` → SPA builds `GET /api/highlights/{video_id}/{output_filename}` to preview in `<video>` + download `[main.py:307-328]`.
74	
75	**4.3 Engine API additions (none for MVP; tagged against real gaps)**
76	| Endpoint | Purpose | Status |
77	|---|---|---|
78	| `POST /api/query` | List rallies (only path) — fix SPA to read `play_segments` | **exists** |
79	| `POST /api/compile` | Stitch selected `segment_ids` → MP4 | **exists** |
80	| `GET /api/highlights/{video_id}/{filename}` | Stream/download compiled reel | **exists** |
81	| `GET /api/config` | Read `stitching.min_shot_count` for the warning | **exists** |
82	| `GET /api/jobs/{job_id}` | Carry `result.video_id` into the picker | **exists** |
83	| `POST /api/compile` → add `download_url` | Return `/api/highlights/...` so SPA stops hand-building it (additive, low-risk; today returns only a filepath `main.py:394`) | **modify** |
84	| `GET /api/health` | Engine-offline banner ping the SPA is specified to use (`HOSTING_PLAN.md:42`) | **new** |
85	| `GET /api/videos/{video_id}/rallies` | RESTful/cacheable alias returning the same `{play_segments}` shape | **new** |
86	| `GET /api/videos` | List a user's videos for a Match picker (only `get_video(id)` exists, `database.py:418`) — needs DB enumerate + identity scoping | **new** |
87	| `GET /api/videos/{video_id}/source` (Range) *or* `/clip?start=&end=` | Stream source/per-span video so a rally previews before selection — neither exists | **new** |
88	| `GET /api/videos/{video_id}/rallies/{segment_id}/thumbnail` | Per-rally poster still (no thumbnail column; WASB frames are transient) | **new** |
89	| `PATCH /api/segments/{id}` | Persist accept/reject/nudge → `candidate_segments.human_label/notes/...` (columns exist, unwritten — corpus loop) | **new** |
90	
91	**⚠ Dead-ends / do-NOT:** never surface `server`/`receiver`/`events` (motion-only randoms `[motion.py:191-198]`); never consume shot-type/player/score (no data); never render a confidence **meter** — `shot_count_confidence` is a Gemini *string* (often `"Unknown"`) and `play_segments` has **no** numeric confidence column.
92	
93	## 5. Risk table
94	| Risk | Severity | Mitigation |
95	|---|---|---|
96	| **No source/clip/frame/thumbnail endpoint** — only compiled reels stream `[main.py:307-328]` | High (UX) | MVP selects on text metadata + previews the *final* reel; preview/timeline deferred to M3 behind a new endpoint |
97	| `min_shot_count` silently drops selected rallies `[main.py:362-383]` | High (looks broken) | Footer warns *before* Build; D7 makes selection explicit; an override is an owner gate (Launch-Report-worthy `[memory: launch-report-convention]`) |
98	| No match-duration field — a timeline must derive `total = max(end_time)`, mis-rendering pre/post dead air | Med | Timeline deferred to M3; never over-promise it as a true scrubber until source streams |
99	| `shot_count` is an *estimate* (`shuttle_exchange_count` w/ `max(3,int(dur/0.8))` fallback) `[gemini.py:470-485]` | Med | Label "approx."; confidence shown as the raw string, not a meter |
100	| No auth / user scoping anywhere (CORS `*`, `videos.user_id` never set/filtered) | High for multi-tester | Fine for single-box local alpha; a ship-blocker for any shared host — gate `GET /api/videos` behind PR-3 scoping |
101	| compile returns a filepath not a URL `[main.py:394]` | Low | Client builds the URL for MVP; `download_url` field (M1) removes the brittleness |
102	| Surface sprawl (query + grid + future timeline + future correction) | Med | Deliberate IA: query+grid = one screen; correction = separate ⭐ corpus screen (owner call, §8) |
103	
104	## 6. Honest limits — what this does NOT do
105	A green SPA build + passing `parseQuery`/selection unit tests prove the **picker logic only** — **not** that the engine's rally boundaries are correct (the measured **rally-END** weakness is a separate quality track, unaffected by this UI). "Conversational" here means a **deterministic four-dimension parser**, not natural-language understanding. Nothing is implemented in `web/` yet — this is `[design]`. The feature curates *what the engine already found*; it cannot surface a rally the detector missed, nor split/merge a mis-bounded one (that's the M4 correction loop).
106

137	## 9. Definition of done
138	- [ ] From a finished job, a user can list rallies, query/select, and download a reel — on **existing** endpoints.
139	- [ ] Only honest fields (ordinal / duration / shot_count+confidence-when-present) are shown; fabricated `server`/`receiver`/`events` are hidden.
140	- [ ] `parseQuery()` covers length/count/order/shot_count, greys out unavailable tokens, shows a visible parsed-pill, and is unit-tested.
141	- [ ] Selection hook is unit-tested; the `min_shot_count` floor is surfaced, not hidden.
142	- [ ] `npm run build` (and `npm test`) clean; the static UI's `data.rallies` bug is gone (or the static UI is superseded by the SPA).
143	- [ ] §7 tracker + this DoD updated; doc archived when P1–P5 land (MVP).

2	// Anchors: backend/infrastructure/database.py:124-135 (play_segments),
3	// backend/main.py:331-396 (query/compile), :307-328 (highlights), :121 (config).

5	export interface RallyMetadata {
6	  /** Gemini's shuttle_exchange_count (fallback max(3, dur/0.8)); absent on some detectors. */
7	  shot_count?: number;
8	  /** Gemini confidence STRING (often "Unknown") — not a numeric meter. */
9	  shot_count_confidence?: string;
10	  detector?: string;
11	  [key: string]: unknown;
12	}
13	
14	export interface RallySegment {
15	  /** The integer the UI selects and passes to /api/compile as segment_ids. */
16	  id: number;
17	  start_time: number;
18	  end_time: number;
19	  duration: number;
20	  /** motion-segmenter FABRICATION (random) — DO NOT surface (see PER_RALLY_SELECTION.md). */
21	  server: string | null;
22	  /** motion-segmenter FABRICATION (random) — DO NOT surface. */
23	  receiver: string | null;
24	  metadata: RallyMetadata;
25	  /** motion-only fabricated sub-events — DO NOT surface. */
26	  events?: unknown[];
27	}
28	
29	export interface QueryResponse {
30	  play_segments: RallySegment[];
31	}
32	
33	export interface CompileResponse {
34	  status: string;
35	  /** server-local path (NOT a URL) — build the download URL with highlightUrl(). */
36	  filepath: string;
37	}
38	
39	export interface EngineConfig {
40	  stitching?: {
41	    /** The shot-count floor /api/compile silently enforces — surface it, don't hide it. */
42	    min_shot_count?: number;
43	    [key: string]: unknown;
44	  };
45	  [key: string]: unknown;
46	}

### 19. user (2026-06-20T16:16:43.921Z)

1	// Pure selection logic over a set of rally ids (no React — node-testable).
2	// One shared Set<segment_id> is the source of truth; the query bar, checkboxes, and
3	// (future) timeline all mutate THIS via these helpers (PER_RALLY_SELECTION.md §4).
4	import type { RallySegment } from "../api/types";
5	
6	export type Selection = ReadonlySet<number>;
7	
8	export const idsOf = (rallies: RallySegment[]): number[] => rallies.map((r) => r.id);
9	
10	/** Toggle one id (returns a NEW set — never mutates the input). */
11	export function toggle(sel: Selection, id: number): Set<number> {
12	  const next = new Set(sel);
13	  if (next.has(id)) next.delete(id);
14	  else next.add(id);
15	  return next;
16	}
17	
18	export const selectAll = (rallies: RallySegment[]): Set<number> => new Set(idsOf(rallies));
19	export const clear = (): Set<number> => new Set();
20	
21	/** Select exactly the rallies NOT currently selected. */
22	export const invert = (sel: Selection, rallies: RallySegment[]): Set<number> =>
23	  new Set(idsOf(rallies).filter((id) => !sel.has(id)));
24	
25	export const selectedRallies = (rallies: RallySegment[], sel: Selection): RallySegment[] =>
26	  rallies.filter((r) => sel.has(r.id));
27	
28	/** Total play-time (seconds) of the selected rallies — what the reel will run to. */
29	export const selectedDurationSec = (rallies: RallySegment[], sel: Selection): number =>
30	  selectedRallies(rallies, sel).reduce((acc, r) => acc + (r.duration || 0), 0);

### 20. user (2026-06-20T16:16:44.426Z)

1	// Small pure formatters (node-testable, no React).
2	
3	/** Seconds → "m:ss" (e.g. 65 → "1:05"). Rounds to the nearest second; clamps negatives to 0. */
4	export function mmss(totalSeconds: number): string {
5	  const s = Math.max(0, Math.round(totalSeconds || 0));
6	  const m = Math.floor(s / 60);
7	  return `${m}:${String(s % 60).padStart(2, "0")}`;
8	}
9	
10	/** "3 rallies" / "1 rally" — count with a correctly pluralized noun. */
11	export function pluralize(n: number, one: string, many = one + "s"): string {
12	  return `${n} ${n === 1 ? one : many}`;
13	}
14

### 21. user (2026-06-20T16:16:44.895Z)

1	// React state wrapper over the pure selection helpers in lib/selection.ts.
2	// Default = ALL selected (D8: curate-by-removal) — the user removes warm-ups.
3	import { useCallback, useMemo, useState } from "react";
4	import type { RallySegment } from "../api/types";
5	import * as sel from "../lib/selection";
6	
7	export function useRallySelection(rallies: RallySegment[], initial: "all" | "none" = "all") {
8	  const [selected, setSelected] = useState<Set<number>>(() =>
9	    initial === "none" ? sel.clear() : sel.selectAll(rallies),
10	  );
11	
12	  const toggle = useCallback((id: number) => setSelected((s) => sel.toggle(s, id)), []);
13	  const selectAll = useCallback(() => setSelected(sel.selectAll(rallies)), [rallies]);
14	  const clearAll = useCallback(() => setSelected(sel.clear()), []);
15	  const invert = useCallback(() => setSelected((s) => sel.invert(s, rallies)), [rallies]);
16	  const isSelected = useCallback((id: number) => selected.has(id), [selected]);
17	
18	  const totalSec = useMemo(() => sel.selectedDurationSec(rallies, selected), [rallies, selected]);
19	
20	  return { selected, count: selected.size, totalSec, isSelected, toggle, selectAll, clearAll, invert };
21	}

1	// The selectable rally grid — one card per play_segment, HONEST fields only:
2	// ordinal (by start order), mm:ss start, duration, and a shot-count chip ONLY when present.
3	// Never renders server/receiver/events (motion-only fabrications, PER_RALLY_SELECTION.md §4).
4	import type { RallySegment } from "../api/types";
5	import { mmss } from "../lib/format";
6	
7	interface Props {
8	  rallies: RallySegment[];
9	  isSelected: (id: number) => boolean;
10	  onToggle: (id: number) => void;
11	}
12	
13	export function RallyGrid({ rallies, isSelected, onToggle }: Props) {
14	  if (rallies.length === 0) {
15	    return <p className="text-ink/60 text-sm">No rallies to show yet.</p>;
16	  }
17	  return (
18	    <ul className="grid grid-cols-[repeat(auto-fill,minmax(150px,1fr))] gap-3 list-none p-0 m-0">
19	      {rallies.map((r, i) => {
20	        const selected = isSelected(r.id);
21	        const shot = r.metadata?.shot_count;
22	        const conf = r.metadata?.shot_count_confidence;
23	        return (
24	          <li key={r.id}>
25	            <button
26	              type="button"
27	              onClick={() => onToggle(r.id)}
28	              aria-pressed={selected}
29	              aria-label={`Rally ${i + 1}${selected ? ", in reel" : ", not in reel"}`}
30	              className={
31	                "w-full text-left rounded-xl border p-3 transition " +
32	                (selected
33	                  ? "border-green bg-green/10"
34	                  : "border-black/10 bg-white hover:border-green/60")
35	              }
36	            >
37	              <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
38	                <span className="font-semibold text-ink">Rally {i + 1}</span>
39	                <span
40	                  aria-hidden="true"
41	                  className={
42	                    "inline-flex h-5 w-5 items-center justify-center rounded-full text-xs " +
43	                    (selected ? "bg-green text-[#04140D]" : "border border-black/20 text-transparent")
44	                  }
45	                >
46	                  ✓
47	                </span>
48	              </div>
49	              <div className="mt-1 text-sm text-ink/70">
50	                {mmss(r.start_time)} · {mmss(r.duration)}
51	              </div>
52	              {typeof shot === "number" && (
53	                <div className="mt-2 inline-block rounded-full bg-paper px-2 py-0.5 text-xs text-ink/70">
54	                  ~{shot} shots{conf && conf !== "Unknown" ? ` · ${conf}` : ""}
55	                </div>
56	              )}
57	            </button>
58	          </li>
59	        );
60	      })}
61	    </ul>
62	  );
63	}

### 27. user (2026-06-20T16:16:55.801Z)

1	// Structured client logger — the observability seed for the operator console
2	// (docs/LOCAL_APPLIANCE_ARCHITECTURE.md §5). Every API call carries a CORRELATION ID so a
3	// single user journey is traceable across the UI → FastAPI → worker. Logs are JSON-shaped
4	// so they aggregate cleanly off a transient host. Keep this dependency-free.
5	
6	export type LogLevel = "debug" | "info" | "warn" | "error";
7	
8	/** A short, unique id to correlate all log lines (and the x-correlation-id header) for one action. */
9	export function newCorrelationId(): string {
10	  const c = (globalThis as { crypto?: Crypto }).crypto;
11	  if (c && typeof c.randomUUID === "function") return c.randomUUID();
12	  // Fallback for environments without crypto.randomUUID.
13	  return `cid_${Date.now().toString(36)}_${Math.floor(Math.random() * 1e9).toString(36)}`;
14	}
15	
16	export function log(level: LogLevel, event: string, fields: Record<string, unknown> = {}): void {
17	  const line = JSON.stringify({ ts: new Date().toISOString(), level, event, ...fields });
18	  if (level === "error") console.error(line);
19	  else if (level === "warn") console.warn(line);
20	  else console.log(line);
21	}
22

### 29. user (2026-06-20T16:16:56.747Z)

1	// Typed client for the engine FastAPI. SAME-ORIGIN relative `/api/*` (the appliance serves
2	// UI + engine behind one origin; in dev Vite proxies `/api` → :8000). Every call carries a
3	// correlation id (header + logs) so a journey is traceable end to end.
4	import { log, newCorrelationId } from "../lib/log";
5	import type { RallySegment, QueryResponse, CompileResponse, EngineConfig } from "./types";
6	
7	const BASE = "/api";
8	
9	export class ApiError extends Error {
10	  readonly cid?: string;
11	  readonly status?: number;
12	  readonly body?: string;
13	  constructor(message: string, opts: { cid?: string; status?: number; body?: string; cause?: unknown } = {}) {
14	    super(message);
15	    this.name = "ApiError";
16	    this.cid = opts.cid;
17	    this.status = opts.status;
18	    this.body = opts.body;
19	    if (opts.cause !== undefined) (this as { cause?: unknown }).cause = opts.cause;
20	  }
21	}
22	
23	interface RequestOpts {
24	  method?: string;
25	  body?: string;
26	  headers?: Record<string, string>;
27	  /** Correlation id to thread through this call; auto-generated if absent. */
28	  cid?: string;
29	}
30	
31	async function request<T>(path: string, opts: RequestOpts = {}): Promise<T> {
32	  const cid = opts.cid ?? newCorrelationId();
33	  const url = `${BASE}${path}`;
34	  const method = opts.method ?? "GET";
35	  log("info", "api.request", { cid, method, url });
36	
37	  let res: Response;
38	  try {
39	    res = await fetch(url, {
40	      method,
41	      body: opts.body,
42	      headers: { "content-type": "application/json", "x-correlation-id": cid, ...(opts.headers ?? {}) },
43	    });
44	  } catch (err) {
45	    // Loud failure, never silent (LOCAL_APPLIANCE_ARCHITECTURE.md §5).
46	    log("error", "api.network_error", { cid, method, url, error: String(err) });
47	    throw new ApiError(`Network error calling ${method} ${url}`, { cid, cause: err });
48	  }
49	
50	  if (!res.ok) {
51	    const body = await res.text().catch(() => "");
52	    log("error", "api.http_error", { cid, method, url, status: res.status, body: body.slice(0, 500) });
53	    throw new ApiError(`${method} ${url} failed: ${res.status}`, { cid, status: res.status, body });
54	  }
55	
56	  const data = (await res.json()) as T;
57	  log("info", "api.response", { cid, method, url, status: res.status });
58	  return data;
59	}
60	
61	/** List a video's detected rallies. POST /api/query {video_id} → {play_segments} [main.py:331]. */
62	export async function listRallies(videoId: string, cid?: string): Promise<RallySegment[]> {
63	  const data = await request<QueryResponse>("/query", {
64	    method: "POST",
65	    body: JSON.stringify({ video_id: videoId }),
66	    cid,
67	  });
68	  return data.play_segments ?? [];
69	}
70	
71	/** Stitch selected rallies into one reel. POST /api/compile {video_id, segment_ids, output_filename} [main.py:342]. */
72	export async function compileReel(
73	  videoId: string,
74	  segmentIds: number[],
75	  outputFilename: string,
76	  cid?: string,
77	): Promise<CompileResponse> {
78	  return request<CompileResponse>("/compile", {
79	    method: "POST",
80	    body: JSON.stringify({ video_id: videoId, segment_ids: segmentIds, output_filename: outputFilename }),
81	    cid,
82	  });
83	}
84	
85	/** The stream/download URL for a compiled reel. GET /api/highlights/{video_id}/{filename} [main.py:307]. */
86	export function highlightUrl(videoId: string, filename: string): string {
87	  return `${BASE}/highlights/${encodeURIComponent(videoId)}/${encodeURIComponent(filename)}`;
88	}
89	
90	/** Read engine config (for the stitching floor). GET /api/config [main.py:121]. */
91	export async function getConfig(cid?: string): Promise<EngineConfig> {
92	  return request<EngineConfig>("/config", { method: "GET", cid });
93	}
94	
95	/** The shot-count floor /api/compile silently enforces — the UI must warn, not hide [main.py:362]. */
96	export function minShotCount(cfg: EngineConfig): number | undefined {
97	  return cfg.stitching?.min_shot_count;
98	}

1	import { describe, it, expect, vi, afterEach } from "vitest";
2	import { listRallies, compileReel, highlightUrl, getConfig, minShotCount, ApiError } from "./client";
3	
4	type FetchFn = typeof fetch;
5	
6	function mockFetch(status: number, json: unknown, text?: string): FetchFn {
7	  return vi.fn(async () => ({
8	    ok: status >= 200 && status < 300,
9	    status,
10	    json: async () => json,
11	    text: async () => text ?? JSON.stringify(json),
12	  })) as unknown as FetchFn;
13	}
14	
15	function lastCall(f: FetchFn): [string, RequestInit] {
16	  const calls = (f as unknown as { mock: { calls: [string, RequestInit][] } }).mock.calls;
17	  return calls[calls.length - 1];
18	}

1	# web/ — KhelSutra Studio (operator console SPA)
2	
3	Vite + React + TS + Tailwind v4. The browser UI that **drives the rally indexer engine**:
4	upload → index → pick rallies → reel → download. See `docs/PER_RALLY_SELECTION.md` (the
5	per-rally curation screen) and `docs/LOCAL_APPLIANCE_ARCHITECTURE.md` (the single-box appliance
6	this SPA fronts).
7	
8	## Dev
9	```
10	npm install
11	npm run dev        # http://localhost:5173 ; proxies /api → http://localhost:8000 (the engine)
12	```
13	Run the engine alongside it: `python -m backend.main --server` (in the badminton-highlight-indexer
14	repo). The SPA uses **same-origin** relative `/api/*` calls — in the appliance a reverse proxy serves
15	this bundle and proxies `/api` to the engine on `127.0.0.1:8000`.
16	
17	## Checks (CI gates merge)
18	```
19	npm run typecheck  # tsc --noEmit
20	npm run test       # vitest run  (unit tests; e.g. the typed API client)
21	npm run build      # vite build
22	```
23	
24	## Layout
25	- `src/api/client.ts` — typed engine client (`listRallies` / `compileReel` / `highlightUrl` / `getConfig`),
26	  grounded in the verified engine contract; every call carries a correlation id.
27	- `src/api/types.ts` — engine data types (honest: `server`/`receiver`/`events` are motion-only
28	  fabrications and must not be surfaced).
29	- `src/lib/log.ts` — structured client logging + correlation ids (observability seed, appliance §5).
30	- `src/App.tsx` — the console shell (screens land in M2+).

### 48. assistant (2026-06-20T16:24:12.646Z)

// Deterministic, dependency-free query compiler for the rally picker (PER_RALLY_SELECTION.md
// D2/D5). NO LLM, NO network — a tiny regex grammar over the ONLY honest, queryable axes the
// engine returns today: rally LENGTH (duration), ORDER (start_time / duration / shot_count),
// COUNT (top/first N), and SHOT COUNT (metadata.shot_count).
//
// Everything else a user might type — shot TYPE (smash/clear/drop…), PLAYER, SCORE — has no
// detector and no column, so we FLAG it as "not available yet" and NEVER fabricate a result.
// Parsing is pure and unit-tested; the QueryBar React component is a thin shell over it.
import type { RallySegment } from "../api/types";

export type CompareOp = ">" | ">=" | "<" | "<=" | "=";
export type FilterField = "duration" | "shot_count";
/** One ANDed predicate over a rally field (e.g. duration > 10s). */
export interface Filter {
  field: FilterField;
  op: CompareOp;
  value: number; // seconds for duration; a raw count for shot_count
}

export type SortKey = "duration" | "start_time" | "shot_count";
export interface Sort {
  key: SortKey;
  dir: "asc" | "desc";
}

/** A recognized-but-unsupported concept — surfaced as a greyed "not available yet" hint. */
export type UnavailableKind = "shot-type" | "player" | "score";
export interface Unavailable {
  token: string;
  kind: UnavailableKind;
}

export interface ParsedQuery {
  raw: string;
  /** ANDed together. */
  filters: Filter[];
  sort?: Sort;
  /** "top N" / "first N" / "N longest". */
  limit?: number;
  /** Concepts we understood but cannot honour (shot-type / player / score). */
  unavailable: Unavailable[];
  /** True when at least one actionable clause (filter | sort | limit) was understood. */
  recognized: boolean;
}

export interface QueryResult {
  /** ALL rallies in display order (sorted if a sort was given, else input order). */
  ordered: RallySegment[];
  /** Ids matching the filters (then limited), in display order. */
  selectedIds: number[];
}

// ── grammar fragments ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
const NUM = "(\\d+(?:\\.\\d+)?)";
// Optional trailing unit/noun. "shots?" is listed FIRST so it can't be eaten by the bare "s"
// time-unit alternative; \b stops "m" matching "minutes" etc.
const UNIT = "(?:\\s*(shots?|seconds?|secs?|minutes?|mins?|min|m|s)\\b)?";

interface OpPattern {
  op: CompareOp;
  re: RegExp;
  /** Which capture group holds the number / the unit. */
  numAt: number;
  unitAt: number;
}

// Leading comparator ("over 10s"). The "no more than" / "at least" guards come BEFORE their
// bare counterparts ("more than" / "less than") so the negated phrase is consumed first.
function lead(alt: string, op: CompareOp): OpPattern {
  return { op, re: new RegExp(`(?:${alt})\\s*${NUM}${UNIT}`, "g"), numAt: 1, unitAt: 2 };
}
const LEADING: OpPattern[] = [
  lead("no more than|at most|maximum(?: of)?|max|up to|<=", "<="),
  lead("no less than|no fewer than|at least|minimum(?: of)?|min|>=", ">="),
  lead("exactly|equal to|equals", "="),
  lead("over|longer than|more than|greater than|above|bigger than|>", ">"),
  lead("under|shorter than|less than|fewer than|below|smaller than|<", "<"),
];

// Trailing comparator ("10s+", "10s or longer", "8 shots or more").
const TRAILING: OpPattern[] = [
  { op: ">=", re: new RegExp(`${NUM}${UNIT}\\s*(?:\\+|or (?:more|longer|over|above|greater))`, "g"), numAt: 1, unitAt: 2 },
  { op: "<=", re: new RegExp(`${NUM}${UNIT}\\s*or (?:less|fewer|shorter|under|below)`, "g"), numAt: 1, unitAt: 2 },
];

function toSeconds(n: number, unit?: string): number {
  return unit && /^m/.test(unit) ? n * 60 : n; // m/min/minute(s) → seconds; else as-is
}

function makeFilter(numStr: string, unit: string | undefined, op: CompareOp): Filter {
  const n = Number(numStr);
  const isShots = !!unit && /^shots?$/.test(unit);
  return isShots
    ? { field: "shot_count", op, value: n }
    : { field: "duration", op, value: toSeconds(n, unit) };
}

const SORT_PATTERNS: Array<[RegExp, Sort]> = [
  [/\b(?:longest(?: first)?|longer first|by (?:duration|length)|duration desc)\b/, { key: "duration", dir: "desc" }],
  [/\b(?:shortest(?: first)?|shorter first|duration asc)\b/, { key: "duration", dir: "asc" }],
  [/\b(?:newest(?: first)?|latest|most recent|recent first)\b/, { key: "start_time", dir: "desc" }],
  [/\b(?:oldest(?: first)?|earliest|chronological|in order|by time)\b/, { key: "start_time", dir: "asc" }],
  [/\b(?:most shots(?: first)?|highest shots?|by shots?|by shot count|shot count desc)\b/, { key: "shot_count", dir: "desc" }],
  [/\b(?:fewest shots(?: first)?|least shots?|lowest shots?|shot count asc)\b/, { key: "shot_count", dir: "asc" }],
];

// Order before kind so a token claimed by an earlier kind isn't double-counted.
const UNAVAILABLE_PATTERNS: Array<[UnavailableKind, RegExp]> = [
  ["shot-type", /\b(smash(?:es)?|clears?|drops?|dropshots?|net ?shots?|drives?|lifts?|serves?|kills?|taps?|pushes?|blocks?|slices?)\b/g],
  ["player", /\b(players?|opponents?|servers?|receivers?)\b/g],
  ["score", /\b(points?|score|deuce|game point|set point|match point)\b/g],
];

// ── public API ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

export function parseQuery(raw: string): ParsedQuery {
  const normalized = raw
    .toLowerCase()
    .replace(/≥/g, ">=")
    .replace(/≤/g, "<=")
    .replace(/,/g, " ")
    .replace(/\s+/g, " ")
    .trim();

  const filters: Filter[] = [];
  let working = normalized;

  // Extract comparator clauses, blanking each match so later passes can't re-read its number.
  for (const pat of [...LEADING, ...TRAILING]) {
    working = working.replace(pat.re, (full, ...groups) => {
      filters.push(makeFilter(groups[pat.numAt - 1], groups[pat.unitAt - 1], pat.op));
      return " ".repeat(full.length);
    });
  }

  // Explicit sort (runs before limit so "top 15 longest" keeps the longest→desc intent).
  let sort: Sort | undefined;
  for (const [re, s] of SORT_PATTERNS) {
    if (re.test(working)) {
      sort = s;
      break;
    }
  }

  // Limit ("top/best N", "first N", "last N", "N longest/shortest", "longest/shortest N").
  let limit: number | undefined;
  let m: RegExpMatchArray | null;
  if ((m = working.match(/\b(\d+)\s+(longest|shortest)\b/)) || (m = working.match(/\b(longest|shortest)\s+(\d+)\b/))) {
    const num = /^\d/.test(m[1]) ? m[1] : m[2];
    const word = /^\d/.test(m[1]) ? m[2] : m[1];
    limit = parseInt(num, 10);
    if (!sort) sort = { key: "duration", dir: word === "longest" ? "desc" : "asc" };
  } else if ((m = working.match(/\b(?:top|best)\s+(\d+)\b/))) {
    limit = parseInt(m[1], 10);
    if (!sort) sort = { key: "duration", dir: "desc" }; // "top N" ⇒ best = longest
  } else if ((m = working.match(/\b(?:first|earliest)\s+(\d+)\b/))) {
    limit = parseInt(m[1], 10);
    if (!sort) sort = { key: "start_time", dir: "asc" };
  } else if ((m = working.match(/\b(?:last|latest|newest|most recent)\s+(\d+)\b/))) {
    limit = parseInt(m[1], 10);
    if (!sort) sort = { key: "start_time", dir: "desc" };
  }

  // Unavailable concepts (scanned on the normalized text; deduped, first kind wins).
  const unavailable: Unavailable[] = [];
  const seen = new Set<string>();
  for (const [kind, re] of UNAVAILABLE_PATTERNS) {
    for (const mm of normalized.matchAll(re)) {
      const token = mm[1];
      if (!seen.has(token)) {
        seen.add(token);
        unavailable.push({ token, kind });
      }
    }
  }

  return {
    raw,
    filters,
    sort,
    limit,
    unavailable,
    recognized: filters.length > 0 || sort != null || limit != null,
  };
}

function rallyValue(r: RallySegment, key: SortKey): number | undefined {
  if (key === "duration") return r.duration;
  if (key === "start_time") return r.start_time;
  return r.metadata?.shot_count;
}

/** Stable sort; rallies missing the sort field always sort LAST (unknown ≠ smallest). */
export function sortRallies(rallies: RallySegment[], sort: Sort): RallySegment[] {
  return rallies
    .map((r, i) => ({ r, i }))
    .sort((a, b) => {
      const av = rallyValue(a.r, sort.key);
      const bv = rallyValue(b.r, sort.key);
      if (av == null && bv == null) return a.i - b.i;
      if (av == null) return 1;
      if (bv == null) return -1;
      if (av !== bv) return sort.dir === "asc" ? av - bv : bv - av;
      return a.i - b.i; // tie → preserve input order
    })
    .map((x) => x.r);
}

function matchesFilter(r: RallySegment, f: Filter): boolean {
  const v = f.field === "duration" ? r.duration : r.metadata?.shot_count;
  if (v == null) return false; // a rally without shot_count can't satisfy a shot filter
  switch (f.op) {
    case ">":
      return v > f.value;
    case ">=":
      return v >= f.value;
    case "<":
      return v < f.value;
    case "<=":
      return v <= f.value;
    case "=":
      return v === f.value;
    default:
      return false;
  }
}

/** Apply a parsed query to the rallies: ordered display list + the ids it selects. Pure. */
export function runQuery(rallies: RallySegment[], q: ParsedQuery): QueryResult {
  const ordered = q.sort ? sortRallies(rallies, q.sort) : rallies.slice();
  let matched = ordered.filter((r) => q.filters.every((f) => matchesFilter(r, f)));
  if (q.limit != null) matched = matched.slice(0, q.limit);
  return { ordered, selectedIds: matched.map((r) => r.id) };
}

// ── human-readable labels (shared by the QueryBar pill and the tests) ─────────
const OP_SYMBOL: Record<CompareOp, string> = { ">": ">", ">=": "≥", "<": "<", "<=": "≤", "=": "=" };

export function filterLabel(f: Filter): string {
  if (f.field === "duration") return `length ${OP_SYMBOL[f.op]} ${f.value}s`;
  return `shots ${OP_SYMBOL[f.op]} ${f.value}`;
}

export function sortLabel(s: Sort): string {
  const key = s.key === "duration" ? "longest" : s.key === "start_time" ? "newest" : "most shots";
  if (s.key === "duration") return s.dir === "desc" ? "longest first" : "shortest first";
  if (s.key === "start_time") return s.dir === "desc" ? "newest first" : "oldest first";
  return s.dir === "desc" ? "most shots first" : "fewest shots first";
  // (key var kept for readability; both branches above are exhaustive)
  void key;
}

/** Human clause labels for the parsed-pill (e.g. ["length > 10s", "longest first", "top 15"]). */
export function summarize(q: ParsedQuery): string[] {
  const parts: string[] = [];
  for (const f of q.filters) parts.push(filterLabel(f));
  if (q.sort) parts.push(sortLabel(q.sort));
  if (q.limit != null) parts.push(`top ${q.limit}`);
  return parts;
}

### 53. assistant (2026-06-20T16:25:43.761Z)

import { describe, it, expect } from "vitest";
import {
  parseQuery,
  runQuery,
  sortRallies,
  filterLabel,
  sortLabel,
  summarize,
} from "./query";
import type { RallySegment } from "../api/types";

const mk = (
  id: number,
  start_time: number,
  duration: number,
  shot_count?: number,
): RallySegment => ({
  id,
  start_time,
  end_time: start_time + duration,
  duration,
  server: null,
  receiver: null,
  metadata: shot_count == null ? {} : { shot_count },
});

// A small, varied corpus: ids ordered chronologically, durations & shot counts mixed,
// rally 4 deliberately has NO shot_count (tests "unknown sorts/filters honestly").
const RALLIES: RallySegment[] = [
  mk(1, 12, 8, 9),
  mk(2, 41, 6, 5),
  mk(3, 63, 21, 19),
  mk(4, 95, 6 /* no shot_count */),
  mk(5, 130, 12, 11),
  mk(6, 158, 29, 24),
];

describe("parseQuery — duration filters", () => {
  it("'over 10s' → duration > 10", () => {
    expect(parseQuery("over 10s").filters).toEqual([{ field: "duration", op: ">", value: 10 }]);
  });
  it("accepts varied units and phrasings", () => {
    expect(parseQuery("longer than 10 seconds").filters[0]).toEqual({ field: "duration", op: ">", value: 10 });
    expect(parseQuery("under 5 sec").filters[0]).toEqual({ field: "duration", op: "<", value: 5 });
    expect(parseQuery("at least 8s").filters[0]).toEqual({ field: "duration", op: ">=", value: 8 });
    expect(parseQuery("at most 8 seconds").filters[0]).toEqual({ field: "duration", op: "<=", value: 8 });
    expect(parseQuery("exactly 7s").filters[0]).toEqual({ field: "duration", op: "=", value: 7 });
  });
  it("converts minutes to seconds", () => {
    expect(parseQuery("over 1 minute").filters[0]).toEqual({ field: "duration", op: ">", value: 60 });
    expect(parseQuery("at least 1.5m").filters[0]).toEqual({ field: "duration", op: ">=", value: 90 });
  });
  it("handles symbols and trailing comparators", () => {
    expect(parseQuery("≥ 10s").filters[0]).toEqual({ field: "duration", op: ">=", value: 10 });
    expect(parseQuery("> 10").filters[0]).toEqual({ field: "duration", op: ">", value: 10 });
    expect(parseQuery("10s+").filters[0]).toEqual({ field: "duration", op: ">=", value: 10 });
    expect(parseQuery("10s or longer").filters[0]).toEqual({ field: "duration", op: ">=", value: 10 });
    expect(parseQuery("8s or less").filters[0]).toEqual({ field: "duration", op: "<=", value: 8 });
  });
  it("does NOT consume 'no more than' as 'more than'", () => {
    expect(parseQuery("no more than 5s").filters[0]).toEqual({ field: "duration", op: "<=", value: 5 });
  });
  it("treats a bare 'Ns' (no comparator) as not a filter", () => {
    expect(parseQuery("10s").filters).toEqual([]);
    expect(parseQuery("10s").recognized).toBe(false);
  });
});

describe("parseQuery — shot_count filters (noun-keyed)", () => {
  it("routes the 'shots' noun to shot_count, not duration", () => {
    expect(parseQuery("over 10 shots").filters[0]).toEqual({ field: "shot_count", op: ">", value: 10 });
    expect(parseQuery("at least 8 shots").filters[0]).toEqual({ field: "shot_count", op: ">=", value: 8 });
    expect(parseQuery("fewer than 5 shots").filters[0]).toEqual({ field: "shot_count", op: "<", value: 5 });
    expect(parseQuery("10+ shots").filters[0]).toEqual({ field: "shot_count", op: ">=", value: 10 });
  });
});

describe("parseQuery — sort", () => {
  const cases: Array<[string, ReturnType<typeof parseQuery>["sort"]]> = [
    ["longest first", { key: "duration", dir: "desc" }],
    ["shortest", { key: "duration", dir: "asc" }],
    ["newest first", { key: "start_time", dir: "desc" }],
    ["oldest first", { key: "start_time", dir: "asc" }],
    ["in order", { key: "start_time", dir: "asc" }],
    ["most shots", { key: "shot_count", dir: "desc" }],
    ["fewest shots", { key: "shot_count", dir: "asc" }],
  ];
  it.each(cases)("'%s' → %o", (input, expected) => {
    expect(parseQuery(input).sort).toEqual(expected);
  });
});

describe("parseQuery — limit (and its implied sort)", () => {
  it("'top 15' ⇒ limit 15, sort longest-first", () => {
    const q = parseQuery("top 15");
    expect(q.limit).toBe(15);
    expect(q.sort).toEqual({ key: "duration", dir: "desc" });
  });
  it("'first 10' ⇒ limit 10, sort oldest-first", () => {
    const q = parseQuery("first 10");
    expect(q.limit).toBe(10);
    expect(q.sort).toEqual({ key: "start_time", dir: "asc" });
  });
  it("'15 longest' / '5 shortest' carry both limit and sort", () => {
    expect(parseQuery("15 longest")).toMatchObject({ limit: 15, sort: { key: "duration", dir: "desc" } });
    expect(parseQuery("5 shortest")).toMatchObject({ limit: 5, sort: { key: "duration", dir: "asc" } });
  });
  it("an explicit sort wins over the limit's implied one ('top 15 longest')", () => {
    const q = parseQuery("top 15 longest");
    expect(q.limit).toBe(15);
    expect(q.sort).toEqual({ key: "duration", dir: "desc" });
  });
  it("combines a filter with a limit ('over 10s top 5')", () => {
    const q = parseQuery("over 10s top 5");
    expect(q.filters).toEqual([{ field: "duration", op: ">", value: 10 }]);
    expect(q.limit).toBe(5);
  });
});

describe("parseQuery — unavailable concepts are flagged, never faked", () => {
  it("flags shot-type words while still applying the available clause", () => {
    const q = parseQuery("smashes over 10s");
    expect(q.filters).toEqual([{ field: "duration", op: ">", value: 10 }]); // honoured
    expect(q.unavailable).toEqual([{ token: "smashes", kind: "shot-type" }]); // flagged
  });
  it("flags player and score concepts", () => {
    expect(parseQuery("opponent rallies").unavailable).toContainEqual({ token: "opponent", kind: "player" });
    expect(parseQuery("game point").unavailable).toContainEqual({ token: "game point", kind: "score" });
  });
  it("does not confuse 'shots' (count) with a shot-type", () => {
    expect(parseQuery("over 10 shots").unavailable).toEqual([]);
  });
  it("a pure unavailable query is not recognized as actionable", () => {
    const q = parseQuery("rallies with drops");
    expect(q.recognized).toBe(false);
    expect(q.unavailable).toContainEqual({ token: "drops", kind: "shot-type" });
  });
});

describe("parseQuery — unrecognized / empty input", () => {
  it("returns recognized=false with nothing to apply", () => {
    for (const s of ["", "   ", "hello there", "asdf"]) {
      const q = parseQuery(s);
      expect(q.recognized).toBe(false);
      expect(q.filters).toEqual([]);
      expect(q.sort).toBeUndefined();
      expect(q.limit).toBeUndefined();
    }
  });
});

describe("sortRallies", () => {
  it("sorts by duration desc / asc, stably", () => {
    expect(sortRallies(RALLIES, { key: "duration", dir: "desc" }).map((r) => r.id)).toEqual([6, 3, 5, 1, 2, 4]);
    expect(sortRallies(RALLIES, { key: "duration", dir: "asc" }).map((r) => r.id)).toEqual([2, 4, 1, 5, 3, 6]);
  });
  it("puts rallies with a missing field LAST regardless of direction", () => {
    // rally 4 has no shot_count → last whether asc or desc.
    expect(sortRallies(RALLIES, { key: "shot_count", dir: "desc" }).map((r) => r.id)).toEqual([6, 3, 5, 1, 2, 4]);
    expect(sortRallies(RALLIES, { key: "shot_count", dir: "asc" }).map((r) => r.id)).toEqual([2, 1, 5, 3, 6, 4]);
  });
  it("does not mutate the input array", () => {
    const ids = RALLIES.map((r) => r.id);
    sortRallies(RALLIES, { key: "duration", dir: "desc" });
    expect(RALLIES.map((r) => r.id)).toEqual(ids);
  });
});

describe("runQuery — end to end", () => {
  it("selects every rally over 10s, in chronological order (no sort)", () => {
    const { ordered, selectedIds } = runQuery(RALLIES, parseQuery("over 10s"));
    expect(ordered.map((r) => r.id)).toEqual([1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6]); // unsorted → input order
    expect(selectedIds).toEqual([3, 5, 6]); // 21s, 12s, 29s
  });
  it("'top 3 longest' sorts all and selects the 3 longest", () => {
    const { ordered, selectedIds } = runQuery(RALLIES, parseQuery("top 3 longest"));
    expect(ordered.map((r) => r.id)).toEqual([6, 3, 5, 1, 2, 4]); // all, longest-first
    expect(selectedIds).toEqual([6, 3, 5]);
  });
  it("a shot filter excludes rallies that have no shot_count", () => {
    const { selectedIds } = runQuery(RALLIES, parseQuery("at least 10 shots"));
    expect(selectedIds).toEqual([3, 5, 6]); // rally 4 (no shot_count) excluded
  });
  it("filter + limit interact (longest 2 of the >10s rallies)", () => {
    const { selectedIds } = runQuery(RALLIES, parseQuery("over 10s top 2"));
    expect(selectedIds).toEqual([6, 3]); // sorted longest-first then capped at 2
  });
  it("an unrecognized query selects nothing and leaves order untouched", () => {
    const { ordered, selectedIds } = runQuery(RALLIES, parseQuery("smashes only"));
    expect(selectedIds).toEqual([]);
    expect(ordered.map((r) => r.id)).toEqual([1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6]);
  });
});

describe("labels", () => {
  it("filterLabel renders honest field + symbol + unit", () => {
    expect(filterLabel({ field: "duration", op: ">", value: 10 })).toBe("length > 10s");
    expect(filterLabel({ field: "shot_count", op: ">=", value: 8 })).toBe("shots ≥ 8");
  });
  it("sortLabel reads naturally for every key/dir", () => {
    expect(sortLabel({ key: "duration", dir: "desc" })).toBe("longest first");
    expect(sortLabel({ key: "duration", dir: "asc" })).toBe("shortest first");
    expect(sortLabel({ key: "start_time", dir: "desc" })).toBe("newest first");
    expect(sortLabel({ key: "start_time", dir: "asc" })).toBe("oldest first");
    expect(sortLabel({ key: "shot_count", dir: "desc" })).toBe("most shots first");
    expect(sortLabel({ key: "shot_count", dir: "asc" })).toBe("fewest shots first");
  });
  it("summarize lists filter, sort and limit clauses", () => {
    expect(summarize(parseQuery("over 10s top 5"))).toEqual(["length > 10s", "longest first", "top 5"]);
    expect(summarize(parseQuery("hello"))).toEqual([]);
  });
});

### 73. assistant (2026-06-20T16:28:52.767Z)

// The fast-entry query bar (PER_RALLY_SELECTION.md P3). A deterministic, OFFLINE parser turns
// plain text ("top 15 longest", "over 10s") into a selection over the SAME shared set the
// checkboxes use. It is honest by construction: a live "parsed-pill" shows exactly how the
// text was understood, and concepts with no data behind them (shot-type / player / score) are
// greyed out as "not available yet" — never silently faked.
import { useMemo, useState } from "react";
import type { FormEvent } from "react";
import { parseQuery, summarize } from "../lib/query";
import type { ParsedQuery } from "../lib/query";

interface Props {
  /** Apply a parsed query to the rally set (App runs runQuery + writes the shared selection). */
  onApply: (q: ParsedQuery) => void;
}

// Each example is a real, parseable query — tapping one fills the box AND applies it.
const EXAMPLES = ["over 10s", "top 15 longest", "shortest first", "at least 8 shots", "first 10"];

const CHIP = "rounded-full px-2.5 py-0.5 text-xs font-medium";

export function QueryBar({ onApply }: Props) {
  const [value, setValue] = useState("");
  const parsed = useMemo(() => parseQuery(value), [value]);
  const clauses = summarize(parsed);
  const typed = value.trim().length > 0;

  const applyText = (text: string) => {
    setValue(text);
    onApply(parseQuery(text));
  };
  const submit = (e: FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (parsed.recognized) onApply(parsed);
  };

  return (
    <div>
      <form onSubmit={submit} className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
        <input
          type="text"
          value={value}
          onChange={(e) => setValue(e.target.value)}
          placeholder="e.g. over 10s · top 15 longest · at least 8 shots"
          aria-label="Filter rallies by length, count or order"
          className="min-w-[16rem] flex-1 rounded-xl border border-black/15 bg-white px-4 py-2 text-sm text-ink outline-none focus:border-green"
        />
        <button
          type="submit"
          disabled={!parsed.recognized}
          className="rounded-xl bg-ink px-4 py-2 text-sm font-semibold text-white disabled:opacity-40"
        >
          Apply
        </button>
      </form>

      {/* Live interpretation — transparent + hand-correctable (no hidden NL guessing). */}
      <div aria-live="polite" data-testid="query-pill" className="mt-2 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-1.5">
        {clauses.map((c) => (
          <span key={c} className={CHIP + " bg-green/15 text-green"}>
            {c}
          </span>
        ))}
        {parsed.unavailable.map((u) => (
          <span
            key={u.token}
            title={`"${u.token}" needs ${u.kind} detection — not available yet`}
            className={CHIP + " bg-black/5 text-ink/45"}
          >
            <span className="line-through">{u.token}</span> · not available yet
          </span>
        ))}
        {typed && !parsed.recognized && parsed.unavailable.length === 0 && (
          <span className="text-xs text-ink/50">Couldn't read that — try “over 10s” or “top 15 longest”.</span>
        )}
        {!typed && <span className="text-xs text-ink/50">Type a quick filter, or tap an example.</span>}
      </div>

      <div className="mt-3 flex flex-wrap gap-2 text-sm">
        <span className="text-ink/40">Try:</span>
        {EXAMPLES.map((ex) => (
          <button
            key={ex}
            type="button"
            onClick={() => applyText(ex)}
            className="rounded-full border border-black/15 px-3 py-1 text-xs hover:border-green"
          >
            {ex}
          </button>
        ))}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

### 76. assistant (2026-06-20T16:29:14.975Z)

import { useMemo, useState } from "react";
import { useRallySelection } from "./hooks/useRallySelection";
import { RallyGrid } from "./components/RallyGrid";
import { QueryBar } from "./components/QueryBar";
import { mmss, pluralize } from "./lib/format";
import { runQuery, type ParsedQuery } from "./lib/query";
import { log } from "./lib/log";
import type { RallySegment } from "./api/types";

// Demo data until the picker is wired to the engine (POST /api/query) in the MVP flow (P5).
// Shape matches the verified play_segments contract; only honest fields are shown.
const DEMO_RALLIES: RallySegment[] = [
  { id: 1, start_time: 12, end_time: 20, duration: 8, server: null, receiver: null, metadata: { shot_count: 9, shot_count_confidence: "High" } },
  { id: 2, start_time: 41, end_time: 47, duration: 6, server: null, receiver: null, metadata: { shot_count: 5 } },
  { id: 3, start_time: 63, end_time: 84, duration: 21, server: null, receiver: null, metadata: { shot_count: 19, shot_count_confidence: "Medium" } },
  { id: 4, start_time: 95, end_time: 101, duration: 6, server: null, receiver: null, metadata: {} },
  { id: 5, start_time: 130, end_time: 142, duration: 12, server: null, receiver: null, metadata: { shot_count: 11 } },
  { id: 6, start_time: 158, end_time: 187, duration: 29, server: null, receiver: null, metadata: { shot_count: 24, shot_count_confidence: "High" } },
];

export default function App() {
  const sel = useRallySelection(DEMO_RALLIES, "all");
  // Display order is separate from selection: a query can re-sort the cards while the shared
  // selection set (sel) stays the single source of truth for what lands in the reel.
  const [order, setOrder] = useState<RallySegment[]>(DEMO_RALLIES);

  // Stable chronological ordinal (by start_time) so re-sorting never renumbers a rally.
  const ordinalOf = useMemo(() => {
    const m = new Map<number, number>();
    [...DEMO_RALLIES].sort((a, b) => a.start_time - b.start_time).forEach((r, i) => m.set(r.id, i + 1));
    return (id: number) => m.get(id) ?? 0;
  }, []);

  const onApplyQuery = (q: ParsedQuery) => {
    if (!q.recognized) {
      log("info", "query.skip", { raw: q.raw, unavailable: q.unavailable.map((u) => u.token) });
      return; // empty / unparseable → leave the user's curation untouched
    }
    const { ordered, selectedIds } = runQuery(DEMO_RALLIES, q);
    setOrder(ordered);
    sel.apply(selectedIds);
    log("info", "query.apply", {
      filters: q.filters.length,
      sort: q.sort ? `${q.sort.key}:${q.sort.dir}` : null,
      limit: q.limit ?? null,
      selected: selectedIds.length,
    });
  };

  return (
    <main className="min-h-screen bg-paper text-ink pb-24">
      <header className="bg-ink text-white">
        <div className="mx-auto flex max-w-5xl items-center gap-1 px-6 py-4">
          <span className="text-xl font-extrabold tracking-tight">
            Khel<span className="text-green">▸</span>Sutra <span className="font-semibold text-lime">Studio</span>
          </span>
        </div>
      </header>

      <section className="mx-auto max-w-5xl px-6 py-10">
        <span className="inline-flex rounded-full bg-green/15 px-3 py-1 text-xs font-semibold text-green">
          per-rally selection · P3
        </span>
        <h1 className="mt-4 text-3xl font-extrabold tracking-tight">Pick the rallies for your reel</h1>
        <p className="mt-2 max-w-2xl text-ink/70">
          Type a quick filter like <em>“top 15 longest”</em>, then fine-tune by tapping cards. Everything starts{" "}
          <strong>selected</strong> — just drop the ones you don't want.{" "}
          <span className="text-ink/50">(Demo data; the live list comes from the engine.)</span>
        </p>

        <div className="mt-6">
          <QueryBar onApply={onApplyQuery} />
        </div>

        <div className="mt-6 flex flex-wrap gap-2 text-sm">
          <button type="button" onClick={sel.selectAll} className="rounded-full border border-black/15 px-3 py-1 hover:border-green">Select all</button>
          <button type="button" onClick={sel.clearAll} className="rounded-full border border-black/15 px-3 py-1 hover:border-green">Clear</button>
          <button type="button" onClick={sel.invert} className="rounded-full border border-black/15 px-3 py-1 hover:border-green">Invert</button>
        </div>

        <div className="mt-5">
          <RallyGrid rallies={order} isSelected={sel.isSelected} onToggle={sel.toggle} ordinalOf={ordinalOf} />
        </div>
      </section>

      <footer className="fixed inset-x-0 bottom-0 border-t border-black/10 bg-white/95 backdrop-blur">
        <div className="mx-auto flex max-w-5xl items-center gap-4 px-6 py-3">
          <span className="text-sm text-ink/80" data-testid="selection-summary">
            <strong className="text-ink">{pluralize(sel.count, "rally", "rallies")}</strong> selected · {mmss(sel.totalSec)} total
          </span>
          <button
            type="button"
            disabled={sel.count === 0}
            title="Reel building wires up in P5"
            className="ml-auto rounded-full bg-green px-5 py-2 text-sm font-semibold text-[#04140D] disabled:opacity-40"
          >
            Build reel ({sel.count})
          </button>
        </div>
      </footer>
    </main>
  );
}

The secondary claim also holds: parseQuery(\"min 5 min\") — first `min` is the comparator, then `5`, then UNIT matches ` min` (the `min` alternative + \\b) → unit=\"min\" → toSeconds(5,\"min\")=300 → duration>=300, correct only by the left-to-right ordering. 54	const NUM = "(\\d+(?:\\.\\d+)?)";

56	// time-unit alternative; \b stops "m" matching "minutes" etc.
57	const UNIT = "(?:\\s*(shots?|seconds?|secs?|minutes?|mins?|min|m|s)\\b)?";
58	
59	interface OpPattern {
60	  op: CompareOp;
61	  re: RegExp;
62	  /** Which capture group holds the number / the unit. */
63	  numAt: number;
64	  unitAt: number;
65	}
66	
67	// Leading comparator ("over 10s"). The "no more than" / "at least" guards come BEFORE their
68	// bare counterparts ("more than" / "less than") so the negated phrase is consumed first.
69	function lead(alt: string, op: CompareOp): OpPattern {
70	  return { op, re: new RegExp(`(?:${alt})\\s*${NUM}${UNIT}`, "g"), numAt: 1, unitAt: 2 };
71	}
72	const LEADING: OpPattern[] = [
73	  lead("no more than|at most|maximum(?: of)?|max|up to|<=", "<="),
74	  lead("no less than|no fewer than|at least|minimum(?: of)?|min|>=", ">="),
75	  lead("exactly|equal to|equals", "="),
76	  lead("over|longer than|more than|greater than|above|bigger than|>", ">"),
77	  lead("under|shorter than|less than|fewer than|below|smaller than|<", "<"),
78	];
79	
80	// Trailing comparator. The unit may sit BEFORE the operator ("10s+", "8 shots or more") or the
81	// "shots" noun AFTER it ("10+ shots") — capture both sides and use whichever is present.
82	interface TrailPattern {
83	  op: CompareOp;
84	  re: RegExp;
85	}
86	const POST_NOUN = "(?:\\s*(shots?)\\b)?";
87	const TRAILING: TrailPattern[] = [
88	  { op: ">=", re: new RegExp(`${NUM}${UNIT}\\s*(?:\\+|or (?:more|longer|over|above|greater))${POST_NOUN}`, "g") },
89	  { op: "<=", re: new RegExp(`${NUM}${UNIT}\\s*or (?:less|fewer|shorter|under|below)${POST_NOUN}`, "g") },

142	export function parseQuery(raw: string): ParsedQuery {
143	  const normalized = raw
144	    .toLowerCase()
145	    .replace(/≥/g, ">=")

156	    working = working.replace(pat.re, (full, ...g) => {
157	      filters.push(makeFilter(g[pat.numAt - 1], g[pat.unitAt - 1], pat.op));
158	      return " ".repeat(full.length);
159	    });
160	  }
161	  for (const pat of TRAILING) {
162	    working = working.replace(pat.re, (full, num, preUnit, postNoun) => {
163	      filters.push(makeFilter(num, preUnit ?? postNoun, pat.op));
164	      return " ".repeat(full.length);
165	    });
166	  }
167	
168	  // Explicit sort (runs before limit so "top 15 longest" keeps the longest→desc intent).
169	  let sort: Sort | undefined;
170	  for (const [re, s] of SORT_PATTERNS) {
171	    if (re.test(working)) {
172	      sort = s;
173	      break;
174	    }
175	  }
176	
177	  // Limit ("top/best N", "first N", "last N", "N longest/shortest", "longest/shortest N").
178	  let limit: number | undefined;
179	  let m: RegExpMatchArray | null;
180	  if ((m = working.match(/\b(\d+)\s+(longest|shortest)\b/)) || (m = working.match(/\b(longest|shortest)\s+(\d+)\b/))) {
181	    const num = /^\d/.test(m[1]) ? m[1] : m[2];
182	    const word = /^\d/.test(m[1]) ? m[2] : m[1];

292	/** Apply a parsed query to the rallies: ordered display list + the ids it selects. Pure.
293	 * An UNRECOGNIZED query (nothing understood) selects nothing and leaves order untouched —
294	 * so garbage input can never silently wipe a user's manual curation. The App also guards on
295	 * `q.recognized` before applying, so an empty box is a no-op end to end. */
296	export function runQuery(rallies: RallySegment[], q: ParsedQuery): QueryResult {
297	  if (!q.recognized) return { ordered: rallies.slice(), selectedIds: [] };
298	  const ordered = q.sort ? sortRallies(rallies, q.sort) : rallies.slice();
299	  let matched = ordered.filter((r) => q.filters.every((f) => matchesFilter(r, f)));
300	  if (q.limit != null) matched = matched.slice(0, q.limit);
301	  return { ordered, selectedIds: matched.map((r) => r.id) };
302	}
303	
304	// ── human-readable labels (shared by the QueryBar pill and the tests) ─────────
305	const OP_SYMBOL: Record<CompareOp, string> = { ">": ">", ">=": "≥", "<": "<", "<=": "≤", "=": "=" };
306	
307	export function filterLabel(f: Filter): string {
308	  if (f.field === "duration") return `length ${OP_SYMBOL[f.op]} ${f.value}s`;
309	  return `shots ${OP_SYMBOL[f.op]} ${f.value}`;
310	}
311	
312	export function sortLabel(s: Sort): string {
313	  if (s.key === "duration") return s.dir === "desc" ? "longest first" : "shortest first";
314	  if (s.key === "start_time") return s.dir === "desc" ? "newest first" : "oldest first";
315	  return s.dir === "desc" ? "most shots first" : "fewest shots first";
316	}
317	
318	/** Human clause labels for the parsed-pill (e.g. ["length > 10s", "longest first", "top 15"]). */

318	/** Human clause labels for the parsed-pill (e.g. ["length > 10s", "longest first", "top 15"]). */
319	export function summarize(q: ParsedQuery): string[] {
320	  const parts: string[] = [];
321	  for (const f of q.filters) parts.push(filterLabel(f));
322	  if (q.sort) parts.push(sortLabel(q.sort));
323	  if (q.limit != null) parts.push(`top ${q.limit}`);
324	  return parts;
325	}

### 218. assistant (2026-06-20T16:54:24.625Z)

Done. Per-rally **P3 is shipped and merged** to `master`, with the observability you asked for plumbed through and adversarially hardened.

## What landed

**[khelsutra#28](https://github.com/avidullu/khelsutra/pull/28)** — the deterministic `parseQuery()` query bar (+ **[#29](https://github.com/avidullu/khelsutra/pull/29)** NEXT_STEPS pointer refresh). Master at `66805ab`, no open PRs.

- **`web/src/lib/query.ts`** — pure, offline `parseQuery()` / `runQuery()` / `sortRallies()` / label helpers over the only honest axes the engine returns: **length / order / count / shot_count**. No LLM, no network.
- **`QueryBar.tsx`** — live parsed-pill + example chips; shot-type/player/score greyed **"not available yet"** and **never faked**. Query + checkboxes share **one** selection set (D3) via a new `useRallySelection().apply`; `RallyGrid` ordinals made stable so a re-sort never renumbers cards.

## Logging & runlogs (your follow-up)

Every P3 action emits a **structured, correlation-id'd JSON runlog** (`lib/log.ts`), documented as a first-class section (`PER_RALLY_SELECTION.md` §4.4) + DoD item:
- One `cid` per query journey: `query.parse` (the exact interpretation) → `query.apply`/`query.skip` outcome.
- **Loud, no silent drops:** `query.empty_result` (**warn** — a looks-empty grid is explained), `query.apply_failed` (**error**).
- `query.unavailable_request` — a **demand signal** for what users ask for that we can't yet do.
- `selection.bulk` + `rally.toggle` trace the manual curation half.

All verified firing live in-browser with correct `cid`s.

## Adversarial review caught real bugs

I ran a multi-agent review before merging — **13 confirmed findings, 4 majors my tests missed**, all fixed and pinned with tests:
- **honesty leak**: `at least 8 smashes` silently became `length ≥ 8s` (pill claimed it) → now drops the clause, only flags it
- **selection wipe**: `top 0` cleared the whole selection → now a no-op
- **unit-blind**: `first 30 seconds` → "first 30 rallies" → now blocked
- **contradictory pill**: `first 10` labeled "top 10" → labels now derive from the actual sort

## Verification
- **62 tests green** (46 in `query.test.ts`), `tsc` + `vite build` clean — the `web` CI job gates all three; remote CI green on both PRs.
- **Live browser CUJ replayed**: filter → sort+limit (stable ordinals) → honest-greying → untick on shared set → gibberish/`top 0` no-op (selection untouched). Zero console errors.
- **Scope: `web/` only — no `site/` change, so no droplet re-sync** (the droplet serves the marketing site, not the SPA). `site/scripts/parity-check.sh` is for `site/` and wasn't applicable.

**Next: P4** (SelectionFooter + `min_shot_count` warning from `GET /api/config`) → **P5** (Build → `/api/compile` → download URL + `<video>` preview), both on existing endpoints. Want me to pick up P4?

342	@app.post("/api/compile")
343	def compile_highlights(req: CompileRequest):
344	    video = db_instance.get_video(req.video_id)
345	    if not video:
346	        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Indexed video record not found.")
347	
348	    # Retrieve matching play segments from db
349	    all_segments = db_instance.query_play_segments(req.video_id, {})
350	    matched_segments = [s for s in all_segments if s["id"] in req.segment_ids]
351	
352	    if not matched_segments:
353	        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="No matching play segments found to stitch.")
354	
355	    # Reject path traversal / absolute output names (os.path.join would otherwise let an
356	    # absolute or '..'-laden output_filename write anywhere on disk).
357	    try:
358	        out_name = safe_output_filename(req.output_filename)
359	    except ValueError as e:
360	        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail=str(e)) from e
361	
362	    # The configured shot-count floor (stitching.min_shot_count) applies on the API
363	    # path too — parity with scripts/run_process_and_stitch.py. Segments WITHOUT shot_count
364	    # metadata are exempt: the floor filters known counts only, so explicitly
365	    # requested manual/legacy segments can't silently vanish under the default floor.
366	    stitching = getattr(global_config, "stitching", None) or StitchingConfig()
367	    kept = []
368	    for s in matched_segments:
369	        meta = s.get("metadata") or {}
370	        sc = meta.get("shot_count") if isinstance(meta, dict) else None
371	        try:
372	            if sc is not None and int(sc) < stitching.min_shot_count:
373	                continue
374	        except (TypeError, ValueError):
375	            pass  # unparseable count -> treat as unknown, keep
376	        kept.append(s)
377	    if len(kept) < len(matched_segments):
378	        logger.info(f"[compile] stitching.min_shot_count={stitching.min_shot_count}: dropped "
379	                    f"{len(matched_segments) - len(kept)}/{len(matched_segments)} segments below the floor.")
380	    if not kept:
381	        raise HTTPException(status_code=400,
382	                            detail=f"All {len(matched_segments)} matching segments fall below "
383	                                   f"stitching.min_shot_count={stitching.min_shot_count}.")
384	
385	    # Extract start/end time pairs
386	    time_pairs = [(s["start_time"], s["end_time"]) for s in kept]
387	
388	    # Run compiler with the configured stitching paddings + optional branding watermark
389	    output_dir = getattr(getattr(global_config, "output", None), "output_dir", "output") or "output"
390	    compiler = VideoCompiler(output_dir, begin_padding=stitching.begin_padding,
391	                             end_padding=stitching.end_padding, watermark=stitching.watermark)
392	    try:
393	        out_path = compiler.compile_highlights(video["filepath"], time_pairs, out_name)
394	        return {"status": "success", "filepath": out_path}
395	    except Exception as e:
396	        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=f"Highlight stitching failed: {str(e)}") from e

### 229. assistant (2026-06-20T16:58:50.491Z)

// The stitching.min_shot_count floor — surfaced, never hidden (PER_RALLY_SELECTION.md D7/D11).
// /api/compile silently drops selected rallies below the floor; the UI WARNS before Build so a
// rally never vanishes without explanation. An override is deferred (owner gate, Launch Report).
import type { RallySegment } from "../api/types";
import type { Selection } from "./selection";
import { selectedRallies } from "./selection";

export interface FloorRisk {
  /** The configured stitching.min_shot_count the engine enforces. */
  minShotCount: number;
  /** Selected rallies the engine WILL drop at compile (numeric shot_count below the floor). */
  atRisk: RallySegment[];
  /** True when EVERY selected rally is below the floor → /api/compile returns 400 (build fails). */
  allDropped: boolean;
}

/** Which SELECTED rallies the engine's min_shot_count floor would drop at compile.
 * Mirrors backend/main.py:362-383 EXACTLY: a rally is dropped iff it has a NUMERIC shot_count
 * strictly below the floor; a rally WITHOUT a shot_count is EXEMPT (kept). Returns null when there
 * is nothing to warn about (no floor configured, empty selection, or none at risk). */
export function floorRisk(
  rallies: RallySegment[],
  selection: Selection,
  minShotCount: number | undefined,
): FloorRisk | null {
  if (minShotCount == null) return null;
  const selected = selectedRallies(rallies, selection);
  if (selected.length === 0) return null;
  const atRisk = selected.filter((r) => {
    const sc = r.metadata?.shot_count;
    return typeof sc === "number" && sc < minShotCount; // exempt when shot_count is absent
  });
  if (atRisk.length === 0) return null;
  return { minShotCount, atRisk, allDropped: atRisk.length === selected.length };
}

### 231. assistant (2026-06-20T16:59:03.911Z)

import { describe, it, expect } from "vitest";
import { floorRisk } from "./floor";
import type { RallySegment } from "../api/types";

const mk = (id: number, shot_count?: number): RallySegment => ({
  id,
  start_time: id * 10,
  end_time: id * 10 + 5,
  duration: 5,
  server: null,
  receiver: null,
  metadata: shot_count == null ? {} : { shot_count },
});

// id1=9 shots, id2=5, id3 (no shot_count), id4=2
const RALLIES = [mk(1, 9), mk(2, 5), mk(3), mk(4, 2)];
const sel = (...ids: number[]) => new Set(ids);

describe("floorRisk", () => {
  it("returns null when no floor is configured", () => {
    expect(floorRisk(RALLIES, sel(1, 2, 4), undefined)).toBeNull();
  });
  it("returns null for an empty selection", () => {
    expect(floorRisk(RALLIES, sel(), 4)).toBeNull();
  });
  it("returns null when no selected rally is below the floor", () => {
    expect(floorRisk(RALLIES, sel(1), 4)).toBeNull(); // 9 ≥ 4
  });
  it("flags only selected rallies with a numeric shot_count below the floor", () => {
    const r = floorRisk(RALLIES, sel(1, 2, 4), 6);
    expect(r?.minShotCount).toBe(6);
    expect(r?.atRisk.map((x) => x.id)).toEqual([2, 4]); // 5<6 and 2<6; id1=9 kept
    expect(r?.allDropped).toBe(false);
  });
  it("EXEMPTS a rally with no shot_count (mirrors the engine floor)", () => {
    // id3 has no shot_count → never at risk, even when selected with a high floor.
    const r = floorRisk(RALLIES, sel(2, 3), 10);
    expect(r?.atRisk.map((x) => x.id)).toEqual([2]); // id3 exempt
    expect(r?.allDropped).toBe(false); // id3 survives → not ALL dropped
  });
  it("flags allDropped when every selected rally is below the floor (compile would 400)", () => {
    const r = floorRisk(RALLIES, sel(2, 4), 6);
    expect(r?.atRisk.map((x) => x.id)).toEqual([2, 4]);
    expect(r?.allDropped).toBe(true);
  });
  it("a lone exempt rally is never allDropped", () => {
    expect(floorRisk(RALLIES, sel(3), 10)).toBeNull(); // only exempt rally selected → nothing at risk
  });
  it("uses strict '<' (a rally exactly at the floor is kept)", () => {
    expect(floorRisk(RALLIES, sel(2), 5)).toBeNull(); // 5 is NOT < 5
  });
});

### 234. assistant (2026-06-20T16:59:19.013Z)

// Sticky compile footer (PER_RALLY_SELECTION.md P4): live selection summary, a reel-name input,
// the Build button, and — above all — an HONEST min_shot_count warning. The engine silently drops
// selected rallies below stitching.min_shot_count; we surface that BEFORE Build so a rally never
// vanishes without a reason (D7). We warn, we do NOT override the floor (D11).
import { mmss, pluralize } from "../lib/format";
import type { FloorRisk } from "../lib/floor";

interface Props {
  count: number;
  totalSec: number;
  reelName: string;
  onReelNameChange: (v: string) => void;
  onBuild: () => void;
  /** A compile is in flight (Build wired in P5). */
  building?: boolean;
  /** Non-null when some selected rallies sit below the engine floor. */
  warning: FloorRisk | null;
}

export function SelectionFooter({ count, totalSec, reelName, onReelNameChange, onBuild, building, warning }: Props) {
  const buildDisabled = count === 0 || building || warning?.allDropped === true;

  return (
    <footer className="fixed inset-x-0 bottom-0 border-t border-black/10 bg-white/95 backdrop-blur">
      {warning && (
        <div
          role="status"
          data-testid="floor-warning"
          className={
            "border-b px-6 py-2 text-sm " +
            (warning.allDropped
              ? "border-red-200 bg-red-50 text-red-800"
              : "border-amber-200 bg-amber-50 text-amber-900")
          }
        >
          {warning.allDropped ? (
            <>
              <strong>All {pluralize(count, "selected rally", "selected rallies")}</strong> have fewer than{" "}
              {warning.minShotCount} shots — the engine skips every one, so the build would fail. Add a longer rally
              or pick more.
            </>
          ) : (
            <>
              <strong>{pluralize(warning.atRisk.length, "rally", "rallies")}</strong> with fewer than{" "}
              {warning.minShotCount} shots {warning.atRisk.length === 1 ? "will" : "will"} be skipped when building
              (the engine's <code>min_shot_count</code> floor).
            </>
          )}
        </div>
      )}

      <div className="mx-auto flex max-w-5xl flex-wrap items-center gap-3 px-6 py-3">
        <span className="text-sm text-ink/80" data-testid="selection-summary">
          <strong className="text-ink">{pluralize(count, "rally", "rallies")}</strong> selected · {mmss(totalSec)} total
        </span>

        <label className="ml-auto flex items-center gap-2 text-sm text-ink/60">
          <span className="hidden sm:inline">Reel name</span>
          <input
            type="text"
            value={reelName}
            onChange={(e) => onReelNameChange(e.target.value)}
            placeholder="my-highlights"
            aria-label="Reel name"
            className="w-40 rounded-lg border border-black/15 bg-white px-3 py-1.5 text-ink outline-none focus:border-green"
          />
        </label>

        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={onBuild}
          disabled={buildDisabled}
          className="rounded-full bg-green px-5 py-2 text-sm font-semibold text-[#04140D] disabled:opacity-40"
        >
          {building ? "Building…" : `Build reel (${count})`}
        </button>
      </div>
    </footer>
  );
}

60

87	      log("error", "query.apply_failed", { cid, source, raw: q.raw, error: String(err) });
88	    }
