1	"""Path-safety helpers for the API boundary (untrusted request fields)."""
2	import os
3	from typing import Optional
4	
5	
6	def safe_output_filename(name: str) -> str:
7	    """Validate an output filename is a BARE name (no dir part, no traversal, not absolute).
8	
9	    `os.path.join(output_dir, name)` silently discards `output_dir` when `name` is absolute,
10	    and `..` segments escape it — so an unchecked `output_filename` is an arbitrary-write
11	    primitive. Returns the name unchanged when safe; raises ValueError otherwise.
12	    """
13	    if not name or not name.strip():
14	        raise ValueError("output_filename must not be empty")
15	    if "\x00" in name:
16	        raise ValueError("output_filename must not contain a null byte")
17	    if "/" in name or "\\" in name:
18	        raise ValueError(f"output_filename must not contain a path separator: {name!r}")
19	    if os.path.isabs(name) or name != os.path.basename(name) or name in (".", ".."):
20	        raise ValueError(f"output_filename must be a bare filename, got: {name!r}")
21	    return name
22	
23	
24	def resolve_under_root(path: str, root: str) -> str:
25	    """Return the real path, asserting it is contained under `root` (raises ValueError if not)."""
26	    rp = os.path.realpath(path)
27	    rr = os.path.realpath(root)
28	    try:
29	        contained = os.path.commonpath([rp, rr]) == rr
30	    except ValueError:  # different drives on Windows
31	        contained = False
32	    if not contained:
33	        raise ValueError(f"path {path!r} escapes the allowed root {root!r}")
34	    return rp
35	
36	
37	def validate_input_path(path: str, allowed_root: Optional[str] = None) -> None:
38	    """Reject null bytes and (when an allowed_root is configured) out-of-root inputs.
39	
40	    With no allowed_root (default, single-user local mode) only the null-byte check applies,
41	    so the local 'point it at any of my videos' workflow is preserved. Setting an
42	    allowed_root (a future hosted/tenant deployment) enforces containment.
43	    """
44	    if "\x00" in path:
45	        raise ValueError("video_path must not contain a null byte")
46	    if allowed_root:
47	        resolve_under_root(path, allowed_root)
48

### 282. assistant (2026-06-20T17:07:24.771Z)

Now the `CompileResult` component (preview + download / error):
// The compiled-reel result (PER_RALLY_SELECTION.md P5): once Build → POST /api/compile succeeds,
// preview the reel inline (<video> over GET /api/highlights/...) and offer a download. The engine
// returns a server-local filepath, NOT a URL (main.py:394), so the SPA builds the URL itself. On a
// failure (e.g. the min_shot_count 400) we show the engine's reason — loud, never swallowed.

export type BuildState =
  | { status: "idle" }
  | { status: "building" }
  | { status: "done"; url: string; filename: string }
  | { status: "error"; error: string };

interface Props {
  build: BuildState;
  onDismiss: () => void;
}

export function CompileResult({ build, onDismiss }: Props) {
  if (build.status === "idle" || build.status === "building") return null;

  return (
    <section
      data-testid="compile-result"
      className={
        "mt-6 rounded-xl border p-4 " +
        (build.status === "error" ? "border-red-200 bg-red-50" : "border-green/30 bg-green/5")
      }
    >
      <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-3">
        <h2 className="font-semibold text-ink">
          {build.status === "done" ? "Your reel is ready" : "Build failed"}
        </h2>
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={onDismiss}
          aria-label="Dismiss"
          className="rounded-full px-2 text-ink/50 hover:text-ink"
        >
          ✕
        </button>
      </div>

      {build.status === "done" ? (
        <div className="mt-3">
          {/* eslint-disable-next-line jsx-a11y/media-has-caption — a stitched rally reel has no captions */}
          <video
            data-testid="reel-video"
            src={build.url}
            controls
            playsInline
            className="w-full max-w-xl rounded-lg bg-black"
          />
          <a
            href={build.url}
            download={build.filename}
            className="mt-3 inline-block rounded-full bg-green px-4 py-2 text-sm font-semibold text-[#04140D]"
          >
            Download {build.filename}
          </a>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-red-800" data-testid="compile-error">
          {build.error}
        </p>
      )}
    </section>
  );
}

import { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from "react";
import { useRallySelection } from "./hooks/useRallySelection";
import { RallyGrid } from "./components/RallyGrid";
import { QueryBar } from "./components/QueryBar";
import { SelectionFooter } from "./components/SelectionFooter";
import { CompileResult, type BuildState } from "./components/CompileResult";
import { runQuery, type ParsedQuery } from "./lib/query";
import { floorRisk } from "./lib/floor";
import { reelFilename } from "./lib/format";
import { log, newCorrelationId } from "./lib/log";
import { ApiError, compileReel, getConfig, highlightUrl, listRallies, minShotCount } from "./api/client";
import type { EngineConfig, RallySegment } from "./api/types";

/** One compact, structured description of how a query was understood (for the trace log). */
function parseTrace(q: ParsedQuery) {
  return {
    raw: q.raw,
    recognized: q.recognized,
    filters: q.filters.map((f) => `${f.field}${f.op}${f.value}`),
    sort: q.sort ? `${q.sort.key}:${q.sort.dir}` : null,
    limit: q.limit ?? null,
    unavailable: q.unavailable.map((u) => `${u.kind}:${u.token}`),
  };
}

// Demo data shown when no ?video=<id> is supplied. Shape matches the verified play_segments
// contract; only honest fields are surfaced. A real session loads from POST /api/query.
const DEMO_RALLIES: RallySegment[] = [
  { id: 1, start_time: 12, end_time: 20, duration: 8, server: null, receiver: null, metadata: { shot_count: 9, shot_count_confidence: "High" } },
  { id: 2, start_time: 41, end_time: 47, duration: 6, server: null, receiver: null, metadata: { shot_count: 5 } },
  { id: 3, start_time: 63, end_time: 84, duration: 21, server: null, receiver: null, metadata: { shot_count: 19, shot_count_confidence: "Medium" } },
  { id: 4, start_time: 95, end_time: 101, duration: 6, server: null, receiver: null, metadata: {} },
  { id: 5, start_time: 130, end_time: 142, duration: 12, server: null, receiver: null, metadata: { shot_count: 11 } },
  { id: 6, start_time: 158, end_time: 187, duration: 29, server: null, receiver: null, metadata: { shot_count: 24, shot_count_confidence: "High" } },
];

type LoadState =
  | { status: "demo" }
  | { status: "loading" }
  | { status: "loaded"; count: number }
  | { status: "error"; error: string };

export default function App() {
  // Job-tethered (D10): a finished job's video_id arrives via ?video=<id>; absent ⇒ demo data.
  const videoId = useMemo(() => new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).get("video"), []);

  const [rallies, setRallies] = useState<RallySegment[]>(videoId ? [] : DEMO_RALLIES);
  const [order, setOrder] = useState<RallySegment[]>(videoId ? [] : DEMO_RALLIES);
  const [loadState, setLoadState] = useState<LoadState>(videoId ? { status: "loading" } : { status: "demo" });
  const [reelName, setReelName] = useState("highlights");
  const [build, setBuild] = useState<BuildState>({ status: "idle" });

  // Display order is separate from selection: a query can re-sort the cards while the shared
  // selection set (sel) stays the single source of truth for what lands in the reel.
  const sel = useRallySelection(rallies, "all");

  // Load real rallies for a job's video (POST /api/query). Default ALL-selected (D8).
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!videoId) return;
    const cid = newCorrelationId();
    log("info", "rallies.load_start", { cid, video_id: videoId });
    let alive = true;
    listRallies(videoId, cid)
      .then((rs) => {
        if (!alive) return;
        setRallies(rs);
        setOrder(rs);
        sel.apply(rs.map((r) => r.id));
        setLoadState({ status: "loaded", count: rs.length });
        log("info", "rallies.loaded", { cid, video_id: videoId, count: rs.length });
      })
      .catch((err) => {
        if (!alive) return;
        setLoadState({ status: "error", error: String(err) });
        log("error", "rallies.load_failed", { cid, video_id: videoId, error: String(err) });
      });
    return () => {
      alive = false;
    };
    // videoId is stable for the page; sel.apply is a stable callback.
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, [videoId]);

  // Read the engine's stitching.min_shot_count so the footer can WARN before a selected rally is
  // silently dropped at compile (P4 / D7). Offline (no engine) is fine: no config ⇒ no warning.
  const [cfg, setCfg] = useState<EngineConfig | null>(null);
  useEffect(() => {
    let alive = true;
    getConfig()
      .then((c) => alive && setCfg(c))
      .catch((err) => log("warn", "config.unavailable", { error: String(err) }));
    return () => {
      alive = false;
    };
  }, []);
  const warning = floorRisk(rallies, sel.selected, cfg ? minShotCount(cfg) : undefined);

  // Stable chronological ordinal (by start_time) so re-sorting never renumbers a rally.
  const ordinalOf = useMemo(() => {
    const m = new Map<number, number>();
    [...rallies].sort((a, b) => a.start_time - b.start_time).forEach((r, i) => m.set(r.id, i + 1));
    return (id: number) => m.get(id) ?? 0;
  }, [rallies]);

  // Build → POST /api/compile → preview/download the stitched reel. One cid traces the whole call.
  const onBuild = async () => {
    if (build.status === "building") return;
    const cid = newCorrelationId();
    const filename = reelFilename(reelName);
    const ids = [...sel.selected];
    if (!videoId) {
      log("info", "build.no_video", { cid, selected: ids.length });
      setBuild({
        status: "error",
        error: "Open with ?video=<video_id> against a running engine to build a reel — demo data can't be compiled.",
      });
      return;
    }
    setBuild({ status: "building" });
    log("info", "build.start", {
      cid,
      video_id: videoId,
      filename,
      selected: ids.length,
      total_sec: Math.round(sel.totalSec),
      below_floor: warning?.atRisk.length ?? 0,
    });
    try {
      const res = await compileReel(videoId, ids, filename, cid);
      const url = highlightUrl(videoId, filename);
      setBuild({ status: "done", url, filename });
      log("info", "build.done", { cid, video_id: videoId, filename, filepath: res.filepath, url });
    } catch (err) {
      const status = err instanceof ApiError ? err.status : undefined;
      const detail = err instanceof ApiError && err.body ? parseDetail(err.body) : String(err);
      setBuild({ status: "error", error: detail });
      log("error", "build.failed", { cid, video_id: videoId, filename, status, error: String(err) });
    }
  };

  // Every query application is ONE correlatable journey: a `query.parse` line records exactly how
  // the text was understood, then a single outcome line (apply / skip / empty) shares the same cid.
  // Unsupported concepts and zero-result queries are logged loudly — never silently dropped.
  const onApplyQuery = (q: ParsedQuery, source: "typed" | "example") => {
    const cid = newCorrelationId();
    log("info", "query.parse", { cid, source, ...parseTrace(q) });

    // Demand signal + honest "we ignored this": surface every shot-type/player/score the user asked
    // for but we can't yet honour (PER_RALLY_SELECTION.md §3 roadmap fields).
    if (q.unavailable.length > 0) {
      log("info", "query.unavailable_request", { cid, source, raw: q.raw, unavailable: q.unavailable });
    }

    if (!q.recognized) {
      log("info", "query.skip", { cid, source, raw: q.raw, reason: "no actionable clause" });
      return; // empty / unparseable → leave the user's curation untouched
    }

    try {
      const { ordered, selectedIds } = runQuery(rallies, q);
      setOrder(ordered);
      sel.apply(selectedIds);
      log("info", "query.apply", {
        cid,
        source,
        filters: q.filters.length,
        sort: q.sort ? `${q.sort.key}:${q.sort.dir}` : null,
        limit: q.limit ?? null,
        total: rallies.length,
        selected: selectedIds.length,
        selected_ids: selectedIds,
        dropped_unavailable: q.unavailable.map((u) => `${u.kind}:${u.token}`),
      });
      if (selectedIds.length === 0) {
        // Loud: a recognized query that matches nothing makes the grid look empty — say why.
        log("warn", "query.empty_result", { cid, source, ...parseTrace(q) });
      }
    } catch (err) {
      // Defensive: the pure parser/selector shouldn't throw, but never fail silently if it does.
      log("error", "query.apply_failed", { cid, source, raw: q.raw, error: String(err) });
    }
  };

  // Manual curation edits are traced too, so a journey (query → hand-tune) reads as one story.
  const onToggle = (id: number) => {
    log("debug", "rally.toggle", { id, action: sel.isSelected(id) ? "remove" : "add" });
    sel.toggle(id);
  };
  const onBulk = (op: "select_all" | "clear" | "invert") => {
    const resultCount = op === "select_all" ? rallies.length : op === "clear" ? 0 : rallies.length - sel.count;
    log("info", "selection.bulk", { cid: newCorrelationId(), op, result_count: resultCount });
    if (op === "select_all") sel.selectAll();
    else if (op === "clear") sel.clearAll();
    else sel.invert();
  };

  return (
    <main className="min-h-screen bg-paper text-ink pb-24">
      <header className="bg-ink text-white">
        <div className="mx-auto flex max-w-5xl items-center gap-1 px-6 py-4">
          <span className="text-xl font-extrabold tracking-tight">
            Khel<span className="text-green">▸</span>Sutra <span className="font-semibold text-lime">Studio</span>
          </span>
        </div>
      </header>

      <section className="mx-auto max-w-5xl px-6 py-10">
        <span className="inline-flex rounded-full bg-green/15 px-3 py-1 text-xs font-semibold text-green">
          per-rally selection · P5
        </span>
        <h1 className="mt-4 text-3xl font-extrabold tracking-tight">Pick the rallies for your reel</h1>
        <p className="mt-2 max-w-2xl text-ink/70">
          Type a quick filter like <em>“top 15 longest”</em>, then fine-tune by tapping cards. Everything starts{" "}
          <strong>selected</strong> — just drop the ones you don't want.
        </p>

        <ModeBanner load={loadState} videoId={videoId} />

        <div className="mt-6">
          <QueryBar onApply={onApplyQuery} />
        </div>

        <div className="mt-6 flex flex-wrap gap-2 text-sm">
          <button type="button" onClick={() => onBulk("select_all")} className="rounded-full border border-black/15 px-3 py-1 hover:border-green">Select all</button>
          <button type="button" onClick={() => onBulk("clear")} className="rounded-full border border-black/15 px-3 py-1 hover:border-green">Clear</button>
          <button type="button" onClick={() => onBulk("invert")} className="rounded-full border border-black/15 px-3 py-1 hover:border-green">Invert</button>
        </div>

        <div className="mt-5">
          <RallyGrid rallies={order} isSelected={sel.isSelected} onToggle={onToggle} ordinalOf={ordinalOf} />
        </div>

        <CompileResult build={build} onDismiss={() => setBuild({ status: "idle" })} />
      </section>

      <SelectionFooter
        count={sel.count}
        totalSec={sel.totalSec}
        reelName={reelName}
        onReelNameChange={setReelName}
        onBuild={onBuild}
        building={build.status === "building"}
        warning={warning}
      />
    </main>
  );
}

/** Pull FastAPI's {"detail": "..."} message out of an error body, else show it raw. */
function parseDetail(body: string): string {
  try {
    const j = JSON.parse(body);
    if (j && typeof j.detail === "string") return j.detail;
  } catch {
    /* not JSON — fall through */
  }
  return body;
}

function ModeBanner({ load, videoId }: { load: LoadState; videoId: string | null }) {
  if (load.status === "demo") {
    return (
      <p className="mt-3 text-sm text-ink/50" data-testid="mode-banner">
        Demo data — open with <code>?video=&lt;video_id&gt;</code> against a running engine to load a real session.
      </p>
    );
  }
  if (load.status === "loading") {
    return (
      <p className="mt-3 text-sm text-ink/60" data-testid="mode-banner">
        Loading rallies for <code>{videoId}</code>…
      </p>
    );
  }
  if (load.status === "error") {
    return (
      <p className="mt-3 rounded-lg border border-red-200 bg-red-50 px-3 py-2 text-sm text-red-800" data-testid="mode-banner">
        Couldn't load rallies for <code>{videoId}</code>: {load.error}
      </p>
    );
  }
  return (
    <p className="mt-3 text-sm text-ink/50" data-testid="mode-banner">
      Loaded <strong className="text-ink/70">{load.count}</strong> rallies from this session.
    </p>
  );
}

// Throwaway stub of the engine API to browser-verify the P5 SPA flow without a GPU.
// Routes mirror the verified contract: /api/config, /api/query, /api/compile, /api/highlights.
const http = require("node:http");
const fs = require("node:fs");
const path = require("node:path");

const MP4 = fs.readFileSync(path.join(__dirname, "tiny.mp4"));
const RALLIES = [
  { id: 11, start_time: 10, end_time: 25, duration: 15, server: null, receiver: null, metadata: { shot_count: 14, shot_count_confidence: "High" } },
  { id: 12, start_time: 40, end_time: 44, duration: 4, server: null, receiver: null, metadata: { shot_count: 2 } }, // below floor 4
  { id: 13, start_time: 70, end_time: 95, duration: 25, server: null, receiver: null, metadata: { shot_count: 22, shot_count_confidence: "High" } },
  { id: 14, start_time: 120, end_time: 130, duration: 10, server: null, receiver: null, metadata: {} }, // no shot_count (exempt)
  { id: 15, start_time: 150, end_time: 168, duration: 18, server: null, receiver: null, metadata: { shot_count: 17 } },
];

const send = (res, code, body, type = "application/json") => {
  res.writeHead(code, { "content-type": type, "access-control-allow-origin": "*" });
  res.end(body);
};

http
  .createServer((req, res) => {
    const url = req.url || "";
    if (url.startsWith("/api/config")) return send(res, 200, JSON.stringify({ stitching: { min_shot_count: 4 } }));
    if (url.startsWith("/api/query")) return send(res, 200, JSON.stringify({ play_segments: RALLIES }));
    if (url.startsWith("/api/compile")) {
      let body = "";
      req.on("data", (c) => (body += c));
      req.on("end", () => {
        const { segment_ids = [], output_filename } = JSON.parse(body || "{}");
        // Mirror the floor: all-below-floor → 400 (e.g. selecting only rally 12).
        const kept = RALLIES.filter((r) => segment_ids.includes(r.id)).filter((r) => {
          const sc = r.metadata.shot_count;
          return sc == null || sc >= 4;
        });
        if (kept.length === 0) {
          return send(res, 400, JSON.stringify({ detail: `All ${segment_ids.length} matching segments fall below stitching.min_shot_count=4.` }));
        }
        console.log(`[stub] compile ${output_filename}: ${kept.length}/${segment_ids.length} kept`);
        return send(res, 200, JSON.stringify({ status: "success", filepath: `/out/${output_filename}` }));
      });
      return;
    }
    if (url.startsWith("/api/highlights/")) {
      res.writeHead(200, { "content-type": "video/mp4", "content-length": MP4.length, "access-control-allow-origin": "*" });
      return res.end(MP4);
    }
    send(res, 404, JSON.stringify({ detail: "not found" }));
  })
  .listen(8000, () => console.log("stub engine on :8000"));

### 345. user (2026-06-20T17:17:55.564Z)

1	# GCP provisioning runbook — DECOUPLED_COMPUTE GPU + storage (asia-south1)
2	
3	Co-located **GPU VM + GCS bucket in ONE region** (asia-south1 / Mumbai) for the first real
4	(non-mock) WASB training + inference. This is the **DO→GCP pivot** (ADR-010): DigitalOcean GPU
5	Droplets are North-America-only (no Singapore/India GPU), so we moved GPU + storage to GCP, which
6	has GPUs in asia-south1 (Mumbai) and asia-southeast1 (Singapore). Owner picked **Mumbai** (closest
7	to Bangalore → lowest latency).
8	
9	> **Cost discipline (read first):** a running GPU VM is the only meaningful cost. Use the cheapest
10	> adequate GPU (**T4**), **STOP or DELETE the VM the moment a run finishes** (a stopped VM bills only
11	> for its disk), and keep the **$100 budget alert** live. `teardown.sh` switches everything off.
12	
13	---
14	
15	## 0. Live resources (created 2026-06-20 — the "switch-off" inventory)
16	
17	| Resource | Identifier | Cost when idle | How to switch off |
18	|---|---|---|---|
19	| Project | `gen-lang-client-0306026826` (billing **Khelsutra Billing** `01420D-419EE0-53D83C`) | — | n/a (shared Gemini project) |
20	| GCS bucket | `gs://khelsutra-rally-corpus` (asia-south1, UBLA) | ~$0.02/GB/mo storage | `gcloud storage rm -r` (⚠ data loss) |
21	| Service account | `rally-worker@gen-lang-client-0306026826.iam.gserviceaccount.com` (`roles/storage.objectAdmin`) | $0 | `gcloud iam service-accounts delete` |
22	| SA key | `~/.gcp/rally-worker-sa.json` (local only, **never** committed) | $0 | delete the file + the key |
23	| Budget alert | `rally-100usd-guard` — $100, alerts at 50/90/100% | $0 | `gcloud billing budgets delete` |
24	| **GPU VM** | **NOT created** — blocked on `GPUS_ALL_REGIONS` quota (see §2) | **~$0.35/hr (T4) running, ~$0.01/hr stopped** | `teardown.sh` (stop or delete) |
25	| APIs enabled | serviceusage, compute, cloudbilling, billingbudgets, iam, cloudresourcemanager | $0 | leave on (free) |
26	
27	Run `bash deploy/gcp/teardown.sh` (see §6) to stop/delete the billable bits between sessions.
28	
29	---
30	
31	## 1. One-time project bootstrap (owner, console — already done 2026-06-20)
32	
33	A bare Gemini/AI-Studio project has the **Service Usage API disabled**, which blocks *all* gcloud
34	provisioning (gcloud's own `services enable` needs that API → chicken-and-egg). These were enabled
35	in the console; recorded here so a fresh project can be bootstrapped the same way:
36	
37	1. **Service Usage API** — https://console.developers.google.com/apis/api/serviceusage.googleapis.com/overview?project=gen-lang-client-0306026826
38	2. **Link a billing account** (a payment method) — https://console.cloud.google.com/billing/linkedaccount?project=gen-lang-client-0306026826
39	3. After (1), the rest enable via gcloud (done): `gcloud services enable compute.googleapis.com cloudbilling.googleapis.com billingbudgets.googleapis.com iam.googleapis.com cloudresourcemanager.googleapis.com`
40	
41	`provision.sh` (§5) is idempotent and re-runs all of step (3) + the bucket/SA/budget.
42	
43	---
44	
45	## 2. ⛔ GPU quota — the one remaining owner action
46	
47	New GCP projects start at **`GPUS_ALL_REGIONS` = 0**, the *global* cap that gates GPU creation even
48	when a regional per-type quota is non-zero. Confirmed 2026-06-20:
49	
50	```
51	asia-south1  NVIDIA_T4_GPUS = 1   NVIDIA_L4_GPUS = 1   (regional: OK)
52	GLOBAL       GPUS_ALL_REGIONS = 0  ← BLOCKS the VM
53	```
54	
55	**Owner:** IAM & Admin → **Quotas** → filter **"GPUs (all regions)"** → Edit → request **1** → submit.
56	(For a single T4 this is usually approved in minutes; sometimes a short review.) Re-check:
57	
58	```bash
59	gcloud compute project-info describe --project gen-lang-client-0306026826 \
60	  --flatten="quotas[]" --format="table(quotas.metric,quotas.limit)" | grep GPUS_ALL_REGIONS
61	```
62	
63	When that reads `1`, run §3.
64	
65	> **⚠ Spot does NOT skip the quota.** TESTED 2026-06-20: a **Spot** T4 create also fails with
66	> `Quota 'GPUS_ALL_REGIONS' exceeded. Limit: 0.0 globally` — `GPUS_ALL_REGIONS` gates **both**
67	> on-demand AND preemptible GPUs, so the increase above is required either way. (A pending request
68	> was filed via the Cloud Quotas API: `POST …/quotaPreferences` `GPUS-ALL-REGIONS-per-project`
69	> → 1.) **Note (2026-06-20 owner decision, ADR-011): GCP is the SERVING path; TRAINING runs on
70	> DigitalOcean (DO credits) — see `SETUP_NEW_MACHINE.md` §2.** Spot is still cheaper once the quota
71	> is granted, and the resumable cache makes preemption recoverable.
72	
73	---
74	
75	## 3. Create the GPU VM (after quota ≥ 1)
76	
77	T4 is plenty for WASB + Gen-0. A local SSD gives a fast `/scratch`. *(The native runner defaults to
78	**streaming** — it decodes frames in-process, skipping the slow cv2 PNG extraction; bit-identical to disk,
79	verified on a real GPU 2026-06-20. Set `indexing.wasb.stream_video=false` only if a container/codec cv2
80	can't seek; `WASB_SCRATCH` still backs the disk path.)*
81	
82	```bash
83	gcloud compute instances create rally-gpu \
84	  --project=gen-lang-client-0306026826 \
85	  --zone=asia-south1-a \
86	  --machine-type=n1-standard-8 \
87	  --accelerator=type=nvidia-tesla-t4,count=1 \
88	  --maintenance-policy=TERMINATE --provisioning-model=STANDARD \
89	  --image-family=ubuntu-2404-lts-amd64 --image-project=ubuntu-os-cloud \
90	  --boot-disk-size=120GB --boot-disk-type=pd-balanced \
91	  --local-ssd=interface=NVME \
92	  --service-account=rally-worker@gen-lang-client-0306026826.iam.gserviceaccount.com \
93	  --scopes=cloud-platform
94	```
95	
96	**Spot variant (cheaper; still needs the §2 quota — Spot is NOT a quota bypass):** same command with
97	`--provisioning-model=SPOT` (replacing `STANDARD`). Add `--instance-termination-action=STOP` to keep
98	the disk on preemption so a re-run resumes. Cheaper, can be preempted.
99	
100	> **Validated image (2026-06-20):** `--image-family=common-cu129-ubuntu-2204-nvidia-580
101	> --image-project=deeplearning-platform-release` ships the NVIDIA driver (no install-on-boot wait;
102	> `nvidia-smi` works immediately) — used for the first real L4 run. `gpu_setup.sh` installs miniconda
103	> on it. **T4 was capacity-stocked-out in `asia-south1-a`; an L4 (`g2-standard-4`, no `--accelerator`
104	> flag — L4 is built into g2) in `asia-south1-c` landed** — try L4/other zones if T4 is unavailable.
105	
106	### 3b. Observability — Ops Agent (GPU graphs + logs in the console)
107	The VM **Observability → GPU** graphs + Logs need the **Ops Agent** (the console's OS-policy install is
108	blocked on DLVM images). Install it directly + grant the worker SA the write roles (it otherwise only has
109	storage), then GPU/NVML metrics + syslog flow to Cloud Monitoring/Logging (tab populates in ~2-3 min):
110	```bash
111	P=gen-lang-client-0306026826; SA=rally-worker@$P.iam.gserviceaccount.com
112	# 1. ENABLE the APIs (bare Gemini project has them OFF — the agent installs but its exports get
113	#    PermissionDenied/SERVICE_DISABLED until these are on; THIS was the empty-graphs cause 2026-06-20):
114	gcloud services enable logging.googleapis.com monitoring.googleapis.com --project $P
115	# 2. let rally-worker publish metrics + logs:
116	gcloud projects add-iam-policy-binding $P --member="serviceAccount:$SA" --role=roles/monitoring.metricWriter --condition=None
117	gcloud projects add-iam-policy-binding $P --member="serviceAccount:$SA" --role=roles/logging.logWriter   --condition=None
118	# 3. on the box (created with --scopes=cloud-platform):
119	curl -sSO https://dl.google.com/cloudagents/add-google-cloud-ops-agent-repo.sh && sudo bash add-google-cloud-ops-agent-repo.sh --also-install
120	# (if the agent was installed BEFORE the APIs were enabled: `sudo systemctl restart google-cloud-ops-agent`)
121	```
122	> Best: bake `curl …add-google-cloud-ops-agent-repo.sh|bash --also-install` into the VM
123	> `--metadata=startup-script` so observability is automatic on every box.
124	
125	> **Long remote steps must be detached.** A plain `ssh … "<long cmd>"` dies if the SSH drops (seen
126	> mid-build, 2026-06-20) — run them `nohup`'d on the box (`nohup bash setup.sh >log 2>&1 &`) and poll
127	> the log, so a dropped connection can't kill the install.
128	
129	> If `asia-south1-a` reports no T4 capacity, try `-b` / `-c`. The Ubuntu image needs the NVIDIA
130	> driver: either add `--metadata=install-nvidia-driver=True` style startup, or after SSH run GCP's
131	> driver installer, **or** use a Deep-Learning-VM image (`--image-family=common-cu124-ubuntu-2204`
132	> `--image-project=deeplearning-platform-release`) which ships the driver. Confirm `nvidia-smi` works
133	> before §4.
134	
135	SSH: `gcloud compute ssh rally-gpu --zone=asia-south1-a`.
136	
137	---
138	
139	## 4. Port the repo + WASB env onto the box
140	
141	The `deploy/digitalocean/` scripts are **provider-agnostic** (they detect the local NVMe / install
142	torch); reuse them on GCP:
143	
144	```bash
145	# from your machine — ship the repo:
146	git archive --format=tar.gz -o /tmp/rally.tgz HEAD
147	gcloud compute scp /tmp/rally.tgz rally-gpu:/root/ --zone=asia-south1-a
148	gcloud compute ssh rally-gpu --zone=asia-south1-a -- 'mkdir -p ~/rally && tar -xzf ~/rally.tgz -C ~/rally'
149	
150	# on the box:
151	cd ~/rally
152	sudo bash deploy/digitalocean/mount_scratch.sh && export TMPDIR=/scratch   # detects the local NVMe
153	./deploy/digitalocean/bootstrap.sh --gpu                                   # venv + torch cu124 (confirm cuda True)
154	. .venv/bin/activate && pip install -e .[storage-gcs]                      # GCS backend
155	bash deploy/digitalocean/gpu_setup.sh                                      # native `wasb` conda env + WASB-SBDT
156	
157	# secrets (NOT in repo): the GCS SA JSON + a rotated Gemini key
158	gcloud compute scp ~/.gcp/rally-worker-sa.json rally-gpu:/root/.gcp/sa.json --zone=asia-south1-a
159	# on the box: export GOOGLE_APPLICATION_CREDENTIALS=/root/.gcp/sa.json
160	mkdir -p /root/.keys && printf '%s' '<ROTATED_GEMINI_KEY>' > /root/.keys/GEMINI_API_KEY && chmod 600 /root/.keys/GEMINI_API_KEY
161	```
162	
163	WASB weights (`wasb_badminton_best.pth.tar`) must be present at
164	`~/models/WASB-SBDT/pretrained_weights/` (gpu_setup.sh does NOT fetch them — stage from Drive/bucket).
165	Point the native runner at them: `export WASB_WEIGHTS=~/models/WASB-SBDT/pretrained_weights/wasb_badminton_best.pth.tar`
166	and `export WASB_PYTHON=$(conda run -n wasb which python)` (or the env's python path). See
167	`docs/TRACKNET_WSL_SETUP.md` for the WASB env. **WASB-C3:** weight provenance is an open legal item
168	before sharing any trajectories/model externally — internal extraction + Gen-0 is fine.
169	
170	---
171	
172	## 5. Stage the corpus + run the first REAL train/infer
173	
174	Config for GCS (no secrets in config — auth via `GOOGLE_APPLICATION_CREDENTIALS`):
175	
176	```json
177	{ "sport": "badminton",
178	  "indexing": { "default_segmenter": "wasb_hybrid", "detector_impl": "native" },
179	  "storage": { "backend": "local", "gcs": { "project": "gen-lang-client-0306026826" } } }
180	```
181	
182	```bash
183	# stage the 7 Done videos + 7 labels into the bucket (from the co-located box — fast):
184	#   videos -> gs://khelsutra-rally-corpus/videos/   labels -> gs://khelsutra-rally-corpus/labels/
185	# (owner handles the actual copy; see "Upload options" below)
186	
187	# FIRST REAL training (real WASB trajectories, native runner):
188	RALLY_DETECTOR_IMPL=native python -m backend.worker train \
189	  --corpus-uri gcs://khelsutra-rally-corpus --out-uri gcs://khelsutra-rally-corpus \
190	  --version 0.1.0 --trajectory-source detector --segmenter wasb_hybrid --config config.json
191	
192	# FIRST REAL inference on the held-out Video 1 (GX010135_proxy.mp4, no labels):
193	python -m backend.worker infer \
194	  --video-uri gcs://khelsutra-rally-corpus/videos/GX010135_proxy.mp4 \
195	  --out-uri  gcs://khelsutra-rally-corpus --segmenter wasb_hybrid --config config.json
196	```
197	
198	### Upload options (how to get a video in + run inference)
199	The worker resolves `--video-uri` via the storage layer — pick whichever fits:
200	- **GCS bucket (recommended, co-located):** `gcs://khelsutra-rally-corpus/videos/clip.mp4`. Upload with
201	  `gcloud storage cp clip.mp4 gs://khelsutra-rally-corpus/videos/`.
202	- **S3 / DO Spaces / R2:** `s3://bucket/clip.mp4` (install `.[storage-s3]`, creds in `~/.aws/credentials`).
203	- **Local upload:** scp the file to the box and pass a bare path / `local:///root/clip.mp4` (identity passthrough, no copy).
204	- **Google Drive (assumes Drive is MOUNTED):** mount Google Drive for Desktop — a fair requirement to
205	  use Drive files locally — and reference them with `gdrive://<path-under-My-Drive>`. The backend resolves
206	  it against your local mount (auto-detected: Windows `G:\My Drive`, macOS
207	  `~/Library/CloudStorage/GoogleDrive-<acct>/My Drive`; override with `GDRIVE_MOUNT_ROOT` or
208	  `storage.gdrive.mount_root`), then it's plain local I/O — **no service account, no API, no extra install.**
209	  E.g. the golden-corpus folder `…/drive/folders/1sHGL7iacnmM80ChT-p2cKy638gK504T_` mounts at
210	  `G:\My Drive\Sharing\Annotation Videos\Golden Label Videos Request - June 15\`, so
211	  `gdrive://Sharing/Annotation Videos/Golden Label Videos Request - June 15/[Done] Video 4 - Badminton/GX020094_proxy.mp4`
212	  (or just the raw `G:\My Drive\…` path — the `local` backend handles that too). ⚠ A **headless GPU VM has
213	  no mount** — there, stage Drive→bucket and read `gcs://`, or `gdown <file-id> -O clip.mp4` to a local path.
214	
215	---
216	
217	## 6. Switch off between sessions (cost control)
218	
219	```bash
220	bash deploy/gcp/teardown.sh stop     # stop the VM (keep disk; ~$0 compute) — fastest resume
221	bash deploy/gcp/teardown.sh delete   # delete the VM (no disk cost; full re-port to resume)
222	bash deploy/gcp/teardown.sh all      # delete VM + SA + budget (keeps the bucket/data)
223	```
224	
225	The **bucket is never auto-deleted** (it holds the corpus). Delete it explicitly only when done:
226	`gcloud storage rm -r gs://khelsutra-rally-corpus`.

240	@app.post("/api/process", status_code=202)
241	def process_video(req: ProcessRequest):
242	    """Submit a processing job (202 + job_id). Poll GET /api/jobs/{job_id} for state.
243	
244	    Fast-fail checks (bad path, missing file, unknown segmenter) happen here so the
245	    client gets an immediate 4xx; everything slow — including MD5 hashing of the file —
246	    runs on the worker (DEFERRED_HARDENING_PLAN #16). The row is created 'queued'; the
247	    worker drains it via `claim_due_job` (EXECUTION_POLICY.md P3 self-scheduling loop).
248	    """
249	    allowed_root = getattr(getattr(global_config, "security", None), "allowed_video_root", None)
250	    try:
251	        validate_input_path(req.video_path, allowed_root=allowed_root)
252	    except ValueError as e:
253	        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail=str(e)) from e
254	    if not os.path.exists(req.video_path):
255	        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail=f"Video file not found at '{req.video_path}'")
256	    if req.segmenter and not segmenter_registry.get(req.segmenter):
257	        available = list(segmenter_registry.list_available().keys())
258	        raise HTTPException(
259	            status_code=400,
260	            detail=f"Segmenter '{req.segmenter}' not found. Available segmenters: {available}"
261	        )
262	
263	    job_id = uuid.uuid4().hex
264	    if not db_instance.create_job(job_id, req.video_path, req.segmenter,
265	                                  req.player_pool, req.max_frames):
266	        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail="Failed to persist job record.")
267	    _get_executor().submit(_drain_due_jobs)
268	    return JSONResponse(
269	        status_code=202,
270	        content={"job_id": job_id, "status": "queued", "poll": f"/api/jobs/{job_id}"},
271	    )
272	
273	
274	@app.get("/api/jobs/{job_id}")
275	def get_job(job_id: str):
276	    """Job state: {status, progress, result, reports}. `status` = execution state
277	    (queued|running|done|failed); the pipeline verdict is result["status"]."""
278	    job = db_instance.get_job(job_id)
279	    if not job:
280	        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail=f"Unknown job_id '{job_id}'")
281	    result = job.get("result")
282	    return {
283	        "job_id": job["id"],
284	        "status": job["status"],
285	        "progress": job.get("progress"),
286	        "video_id": job.get("video_id"),
287	        "error": job.get("error"),
288	        "result": result,
289	        "reports": (result or {}).get("reports"),
290	        "created_at": job.get("created_at"),
291	        "started_at": job.get("started_at"),
292	        "finished_at": job.get("finished_at"),
293	    }

### 363. assistant (2026-06-20T17:26:55.070Z)

// Serves the REAL GPU-run artifacts (22 WASB rallies + the real stitched reel) through the
// verified engine API contract, so the SPA exercises the true load→select→compile→preview flow
// against authentic data. (The live GPU is busy with the engine session's M2 training; these are
// the genuine outputs of the first L4 run — intervals.csv + highlight_smoke_l4.mp4.)
const http = require("node:http");
const fs = require("node:fs");
const path = require("node:path");

const REEL = fs.readFileSync(path.join(__dirname, "reel.mp4"));

// Parse the real intervals.csv (start,end,shot_count,ending_reason) → play_segments.
// shot_count is empty (native WASB doesn't emit it) → metadata {} (honest: no shot chip, no floor).
const csv = fs.readFileSync(path.join(__dirname, "intervals.csv"), "utf8").trim().split(/\r?\n/);
const RALLIES = csv.slice(1).map((line, i) => {
  const [start, end] = line.split(",");
  const s = Number(start), e = Number(end);
  return { id: i + 1, start_time: s, end_time: e, duration: +(e - s).toFixed(3), server: null, receiver: null, metadata: {} };
});
console.log(`[stub] ${RALLIES.length} real rallies loaded from intervals.csv`);

const send = (res, code, body, type = "application/json") => {
  res.writeHead(code, { "content-type": type, "access-control-allow-origin": "*" });
  res.end(body);
};

http
  .createServer((req, res) => {
    const url = req.url || "";
    if (url.startsWith("/api/config")) return send(res, 200, JSON.stringify({ stitching: { min_shot_count: 4 } }));
    if (url.startsWith("/api/query")) return send(res, 200, JSON.stringify({ play_segments: RALLIES }));
    if (url.startsWith("/api/compile")) {
      let body = "";
      req.on("data", (c) => (body += c));
      req.on("end", () => {
        const { segment_ids = [], output_filename } = JSON.parse(body || "{}");
        console.log(`[stub] compile ${output_filename}: ${segment_ids.length} rallies → reel`);
        send(res, 200, JSON.stringify({ status: "success", filepath: `/out/${output_filename}` }));
      });
      return;
    }
    if (url.startsWith("/api/highlights/")) {
      res.writeHead(200, { "content-type": "video/mp4", "content-length": REEL.length, "access-control-allow-origin": "*" });
      return res.end(REEL);
    }
    send(res, 404, JSON.stringify({ detail: "not found" }));
  })
  .listen(8000, () => console.log("[stub] real-artifact engine on :8000"));

### 373. assistant (2026-06-20T17:29:23.820Z)

// Records a promo screencast of the KhelSutra Studio per-rally CUJ, driven against the REAL
// GPU-run data (22 WASB rallies + the real stitched reel, served by stub-real.js). Playwright
// records the page to webm; ffmpeg converts to mp4 afterwards.
const { chromium } = require("playwright");

const SPA = "http://localhost:5180/?video=05e0e577029daafcf425ca70c0c4f0a3";
const W = 1280, H = 800;
const hold = (ms) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms));

(async () => {
  const browser = await chromium.launch();
  const context = await browser.newContext({
    viewport: { width: W, height: H },
    recordVideo: { dir: __dirname + "/video", size: { width: W, height: H } },
    deviceScaleFactor: 2,
  });
  const page = await context.newPage();

  await page.goto(SPA, { waitUntil: "networkidle" });
  await page.getByTestId("mode-banner").filter({ hasText: "Loaded" }).waitFor({ timeout: 15000 });
  await hold(2600); // landing: 22 rallies, all selected

  const q = page.getByLabel(/Filter rallies/);

  // 1) Fast query: "top 8 longest"
  await q.click();
  await q.pressSequentially("top 8 longest", { delay: 85 });
  await hold(1900); // show the parsed pill (longest first · top 8)
  await q.press("Enter");
  await hold(2400); // grid reorders longest-first, footer recomputes to 8

  // 2) Honesty moment: an unsupported concept is flagged, never faked
  await q.fill("");
  await q.pressSequentially("smashes over 4s", { delay: 85 });
  await hold(2600); // green "length > 4s" + greyed "smashes · not available yet"
  await q.press("Enter");
  await hold(2000); // applies length>4s only

  // 3) Name the reel
  const name = page.getByLabel("Reel name");
  await name.click();
  await name.fill("");
  await name.pressSequentially("tuesday-long-points", { delay: 60 });
  await hold(1100);

  // 4) Build → preview the real reel + download
  await page.getByRole("button", { name: /Build reel/ }).click();
  await page.getByTestId("compile-result").waitFor({ timeout: 20000 });
  await page.getByTestId("compile-result").scrollIntoViewIfNeeded();
  await hold(4000); // "Your reel is ready" + <video> preview + download
  await page.getByTestId("reel-video").scrollIntoViewIfNeeded();
  await hold(2500);

  const video = page.video();
  await context.close();
  await browser.close();
  const p = await video.path();
  console.log("VIDEO:" + p);
})().catch((e) => {
  console.error("CAPTURE FAILED:", e);
  process.exit(1);
});

When I went to provision the T4 you approved, I found a **running GPU VM already there**: `rally-gpu` (L4) was **mid-M2-training** (the concurrent engine session's `worker train --version 0.1.0-l4`, ~57 min in). 1	// The selectable rally grid — one card per play_segment, HONEST fields only:
2	// ordinal (by start order), mm:ss start, duration, and a shot-count chip ONLY when present.
3	// Never renders server/receiver/events (motion-only fabrications, PER_RALLY_SELECTION.md §4).
4	import type { RallySegment } from "../api/types";
5	import { mmss } from "../lib/format";
6	
7	interface Props {
8	  rallies: RallySegment[];
9	  isSelected: (id: number) => boolean;
10	  onToggle: (id: number) => void;
11	  /** Stable chronological label for a rally (by start_time), so re-sorting via a query
12	   * doesn't renumber the cards. Falls back to display position when absent. */
13	  ordinalOf?: (id: number) => number;
14	}
15	
16	export function RallyGrid({ rallies, isSelected, onToggle, ordinalOf }: Props) {
17	  if (rallies.length === 0) {
18	    return <p className="text-ink/60 text-sm">No rallies to show yet.</p>;
19	  }
20	  return (
21	    <ul className="grid grid-cols-[repeat(auto-fill,minmax(150px,1fr))] gap-3 list-none p-0 m-0">
22	      {rallies.map((r, i) => {
23	        const selected = isSelected(r.id);
24	        const n = ordinalOf ? ordinalOf(r.id) : i + 1;
25	        const shot = r.metadata?.shot_count;
26	        const conf = r.metadata?.shot_count_confidence;
27	        return (
28	          <li key={r.id}>
29	            <button
30	              type="button"
31	              onClick={() => onToggle(r.id)}
32	              aria-pressed={selected}
33	              aria-label={`Rally ${n}${selected ? ", in reel" : ", not in reel"}`}
34	              className={
35	                "w-full text-left rounded-xl border p-3 transition " +
36	                (selected
37	                  ? "border-green bg-green/10"
38	                  : "border-black/10 bg-white hover:border-green/60")
39	              }
40	            >
41	              <div className="flex items-center justify-between">

46	                    "inline-flex h-5 w-5 items-center justify-center rounded-full text-xs " +

48	                  }
49	                >
50	                  ✓
51	                </span>
52	              </div>
53	              <div className="mt-1 text-sm text-ink/70">
54	                {mmss(r.start_time)} · {mmss(r.duration)}
55	              </div>
56	              {typeof shot === "number" && (
57	                <div className="mt-2 inline-block rounded-full bg-paper px-2 py-0.5 text-xs text-ink/70">
58	                  ~{shot} shots{conf && conf !== "Unknown" ? ` · ${conf}` : ""}
59	                </div>
60	              )}
61	            </button>
62	          </li>

64	      })}
65	    </ul>

### 407. user (2026-06-20T17:40:40.403Z)

1	// The fast-entry query bar (PER_RALLY_SELECTION.md P3). A deterministic, OFFLINE parser turns
2	// plain text ("top 15 longest", "over 10s") into a selection over the SAME shared set the
3	// checkboxes use. It is honest by construction: a live "parsed-pill" shows exactly how the
4	// text was understood, and concepts with no data behind them (shot-type / player / score) are
5	// greyed out as "not available yet" — never silently faked.
6	import { useMemo, useState } from "react";
7	import type { FormEvent } from "react";
8	import { parseQuery, summarize } from "../lib/query";
9	import type { ParsedQuery } from "../lib/query";
10	
11	interface Props {
12	  /** Apply a parsed query to the rally set (App runs runQuery + writes the shared selection).
13	   * `source` distinguishes a typed-and-submitted query from an example-chip tap (for the trace). */
14	  onApply: (q: ParsedQuery, source: "typed" | "example") => void;
15	}
16	
17	// Each example is a real, parseable query — tapping one fills the box AND applies it.
18	const EXAMPLES = ["over 10s", "top 15 longest", "shortest first", "at least 8 shots", "first 10"];
19	
20	const CHIP = "rounded-full px-2.5 py-0.5 text-xs font-medium";
21	
22	export function QueryBar({ onApply }: Props) {
23	  const [value, setValue] = useState("");
24	  const parsed = useMemo(() => parseQuery(value), [value]);
25	  const clauses = summarize(parsed);
26	  const typed = value.trim().length > 0;
27	
28	  const applyText = (text: string) => {
29	    setValue(text);
30	    onApply(parseQuery(text), "example");
31	  };
32	  const submit = (e: FormEvent) => {
33	    e.preventDefault();
34	    if (parsed.recognized) onApply(parsed, "typed");
35	  };
36	
37	  return (
38	    <div>
39	      <form onSubmit={submit} className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
40	        <input
41	          type="text"
42	          value={value}
43	          onChange={(e) => setValue(e.target.value)}
44	          placeholder="e.g. over 10s · top 15 longest · at least 8 shots"
45	          aria-label="Filter rallies by length, count or order"
46	          className="min-w-[16rem] flex-1 rounded-xl border border-black/15 bg-white px-4 py-2 text-sm text-ink outline-none focus:border-green"
47	        />
48	        <button
49	          type="submit"
50	          disabled={!parsed.recognized}
51	          className="rounded-xl bg-ink px-4 py-2 text-sm font-semibold text-white disabled:opacity-40"
52	        >
53	          Apply
54	        </button>
55	      </form>
56	
57	      {/* Live interpretation — transparent + hand-correctable (no hidden NL guessing). */}
58	      <div aria-live="polite" data-testid="query-pill" className="mt-2 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-1.5">
59	        {clauses.map((c) => (
60	          <span key={c} className={CHIP + " bg-green/15 text-green"}>
61	            {c}
62	          </span>
63	        ))}
64	        {parsed.unavailable.map((u) => (
65	          <span
66	            key={u.token}
67	            title={`"${u.token}" needs ${u.kind} detection — not available yet`}
68	            className={CHIP + " bg-black/5 text-ink/45"}
69	          >
70	            <span className="line-through">{u.token}</span> · not available yet
71	          </span>
72	        ))}
73	        {typed && !parsed.recognized && parsed.unavailable.length === 0 && (
74	          <span className="text-xs text-ink/50">Couldn't read that — try “over 10s” or “top 15 longest”.</span>
75	        )}
76	        {!typed && <span className="text-xs text-ink/50">Type a quick filter, or tap an example.</span>}
77	      </div>
78	
79	      <div className="mt-3 flex flex-wrap gap-2 text-sm">
80	        <span className="text-ink/40">Try:</span>
81	        {EXAMPLES.map((ex) => (
82	          <button
83	            key={ex}
84	            type="button"
85	            onClick={() => applyText(ex)}
86	            className="rounded-full border border-black/15 px-3 py-1 text-xs hover:border-green"
87	          >
88	            {ex}
89	          </button>
90	        ))}
91	      </div>
92	    </div>
93	  );
94	}
95

### 426. assistant (2026-06-20T17:42:41.833Z)

import { describe, it, expect } from "vitest";
import { EXAMPLES } from "./QueryBar";
import { parseQuery } from "../lib/query";

// The example chips apply on tap, so a broken example would silently do nothing (or show a
// "couldn't read that"). Pin that EVERY shipped example is a recognized, fully-available query
// (no unsupported tokens) — a regression guard for the duration-led example set.
describe("QueryBar examples", () => {
  it("are all recognized, actionable, and fully available (no experimental/unsupported tokens)", () => {
    for (const ex of EXAMPLES) {
      const q = parseQuery(ex);
      expect(q.recognized, ex).toBe(true);
      expect(q.unavailable, ex).toEqual([]);
      // every example must actually constrain or order the set
      expect(q.filters.length > 0 || q.sort != null || q.limit != null, ex).toBe(true);
    }
  });

  it("lead with rally length, not the experimental shot-count axis", () => {
    const shotExamples = EXAMPLES.filter((ex) => parseQuery(ex).filters.some((f) => f.field === "shot_count"));
    expect(shotExamples).toEqual([]);
  });
});

#33 merged (master `155153d`). Now the **GCS round-trip** (task 11): pull a high-rally video + its intervals from the bucket → stitch a curated "top-10 longest" highlight → put it back in the bucket. Reusing artifacts, CPU-only (ffmpeg, the same operation the engine's stitcher performs).

### 438. assistant (2026-06-20T17:45:31.399Z)

// Reuse the real GPU-run artifacts to build a CURATED highlight (top-10 longest rallies) and put
// it back in GCS — demonstrating the GCS-in → highlights → GCS-out flow without a GPU. ffmpeg does
// the same per-clip-extract → concat the engine's VideoCompiler does.
const { execFileSync } = require("node:child_process");
const fs = require("node:fs");
const path = require("node:path");

const DIR = __dirname;
const SRC = path.join(DIR, "source.mp4");
const OUT = path.join(DIR, "curated_top10_longest.mp4");
const PAD = 0.3; // small pre/post roll (engine uses begin/end padding)

// Parse intervals.csv (start,end,shot_count,ending_reason)
const rows = fs.readFileSync(path.join(DIR, "intervals.csv"), "utf8").trim().split(/\r?\n/).slice(1);
const all = rows.map((l, i) => {
  const [s, e] = l.split(",").map(Number);
  return { i: i + 1, start: s, end: e, dur: e - s };
});
// Top 10 longest, then play them back in chronological order.
const top = [...all].sort((a, b) => b.dur - a.dur).slice(0, 10).sort((a, b) => a.start - b.start);
console.log(`[stitch] ${all.length} rallies → top ${top.length} longest:`,
  top.map((r) => `#${r.i}(${r.dur.toFixed(1)}s)`).join(" "));

// Extract each clip (re-encode to a uniform codec so concat is clean).
const listFile = path.join(DIR, "concat.txt");
const lines = [];
top.forEach((r, n) => {
  const clip = path.join(DIR, `clip_${String(n).padStart(2, "0")}.mp4`);
  const ss = Math.max(0, r.start - PAD);
  const t = r.dur + 2 * PAD;
  execFileSync("ffmpeg", ["-y", "-ss", String(ss), "-i", SRC, "-t", String(t),
    "-c:v", "libx264", "-preset", "veryfast", "-crf", "22", "-pix_fmt", "yuv420p",
    "-c:a", "aac", "-ar", "48000", "-vsync", "cfr", clip], { stdio: "ignore" });
  lines.push(`file '${clip.replace(/\\/g, "/")}'`);
});
fs.writeFileSync(listFile, lines.join("\n"));

// Concat the clips into one reel.
execFileSync("ffmpeg", ["-y", "-f", "concat", "-safe", "0", "-i", listFile,
  "-c:v", "libx264", "-preset", "veryfast", "-crf", "22", "-pix_fmt", "yuv420p",
  "-c:a", "aac", "-movflags", "+faststart", OUT], { stdio: "ignore" });

const sz = fs.statSync(OUT).size;
const total = top.reduce((a, r) => a + r.dur + 2 * PAD, 0);
console.log(`[stitch] ${OUT} — ${top.length} rallies, ~${total.toFixed(1)}s, ${(sz / 1e6).toFixed(2)}MB`);

### 458. assistant (2026-06-20T17:49:39.748Z)

// Stub for the TOOL screencast: real 22 WASB rallies (for the grid) + a FOOTAGE-FREE branded
// placeholder for the reel preview (so the self-hosted screencast contains no faces).
const http = require("node:http"), fs = require("node:fs"), path = require("node:path");
const REEL = fs.readFileSync(path.join(__dirname, "placeholder.mp4"));
const csv = fs.readFileSync(path.join(__dirname, "intervals.csv"), "utf8").trim().split(/\r?\n/);
const RALLIES = csv.slice(1).map((l, i) => {
  const [s, e] = l.split(",");
  return { id: i + 1, start_time: +s, end_time: +e, duration: +(+e - +s).toFixed(3), server: null, receiver: null, metadata: {} };
});
const send = (res, c, b, t = "application/json") => { res.writeHead(c, { "content-type": t, "access-control-allow-origin": "*" }); res.end(b); };
http.createServer((req, res) => {
  const u = req.url || "";
  if (u.startsWith("/api/config")) return send(res, 200, JSON.stringify({ stitching: { min_shot_count: 4 } }));
  if (u.startsWith("/api/query")) return send(res, 200, JSON.stringify({ play_segments: RALLIES }));
  if (u.startsWith("/api/compile")) { let b = ""; req.on("data", (c) => (b += c)); req.on("end", () => send(res, 200, JSON.stringify({ status: "success", filepath: "/out/" + (JSON.parse(b || "{}").output_filename || "reel.mp4") }))); return; }
  if (u.startsWith("/api/highlights/")) { res.writeHead(200, { "content-type": "video/mp4", "content-length": REEL.length, "access-control-allow-origin": "*" }); return res.end(REEL); }
  send(res, 404, "{}");
}).listen(8000, () => console.log("[stub-tool] :8000 — 22 real rallies + footage-free reel"));

### 460. assistant (2026-06-20T17:49:52.540Z)

// Records the TOOL screencast against the duration-first SPA, with a footage-free reel preview.
const { chromium } = require("playwright");
const SPA = "http://localhost:5181/?video=05e0e577029daafcf425ca70c0c4f0a3";
const W = 1280, H = 800;
const hold = (ms) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms));

(async () => {
  const browser = await chromium.launch();
  const context = await browser.newContext({
    viewport: { width: W, height: H },
    recordVideo: { dir: __dirname + "/toolvid", size: { width: W, height: H } },
    deviceScaleFactor: 2,
  });
  const page = await context.newPage();
  await page.goto(SPA, { waitUntil: "networkidle" });
  await page.getByTestId("mode-banner").filter({ hasText: "Loaded" }).waitFor({ timeout: 15000 });
  await hold(2600); // landing: 22 real rallies, length-first, all selected

  const q = page.getByLabel(/Filter rallies/);
  await q.click();
  await q.pressSequentially("top 8 longest", { delay: 85 });
  await hold(1900);              // parsed pill: longest first · top 8
  await q.press("Enter");
  await hold(2300);              // reorders longest-first, 8 selected

  await q.fill("");
  await q.pressSequentially("smashes over 4s", { delay: 85 });
  await hold(2600);              // honest: green "length > 4s" + greyed "smashes · not available yet"
  await q.press("Enter");
  await hold(1900);

  const name = page.getByLabel("Reel name");
  await name.click(); await name.fill(""); await name.pressSequentially("tuesday-long-points", { delay: 55 });
  await hold(1000);

  await page.getByRole("button", { name: /Build reel/ }).click();
  await page.getByTestId("compile-result").waitFor({ timeout: 20000 });
  await page.getByTestId("compile-result").scrollIntoViewIfNeeded();
  await hold(3800);              // "Your reel is ready" — footage-free branded preview + download
  await page.getByTestId("reel-video").scrollIntoViewIfNeeded();
  await hold(2400);

  const video = page.video();
  await context.close();
  await browser.close();
  console.log("VIDEO:" + (await video.path()));
})().catch((e) => { console.error("CAPTURE FAILED:", e); process.exit(1); });

# Asserts the two live hosts (Cloudflare + droplet) serve BYTE-IDENTICAL content.

### 577. assistant (2026-06-20T18:13:01.967Z)

---
name: machine-pooling-idea
description: "Owner 'food for thought' (2026-06-20): pool GPU machines for shared/queued use instead of spin-up/kill per task — born from a real single-GPU-slot collision between two sessions."
metadata:
  node_type: memory
  type: project
---

**Owner observation (2026-06-20):** *"the other VM is about to be deleted by another session and I guess I need to find a way to **pool these machines rather than spinup/kill for each work**. Food for thought."*

**What triggered it:** when this session went to spin up a T4 for the per-rally **CUJ-validation** run (owner-approved), the **single GPU slot was already taken** — `GPUS_ALL_REGIONS = 1` and the concurrent **DECOUPLED_COMPUTE session** had `rally-gpu` (`g2-standard-4`/L4, asia-southeast1-a) mid **M2 training**. So I **could not provision a second GPU** (quota) and **would not disrupt** their job → I validated the CUJ against the prior run's REAL artifacts instead (intervals + reel from the bucket). Two parallel sessions contending for one per-task VM is the friction the owner is flagging.

**Why it matters:** per-task spin-up/kill (the current `deploy/gcp/{provision,teardown}.sh` flow) is clean for cost (idle = $0) but **serializes GPU work across sessions** at quota=1 and adds spin-up + env-build latency every time (the `gpu_setup.sh` conda/WASB build is the slow part).

**Options to weigh (not yet decided — food for thought):**
- **Raise the quota** (`GPUS_ALL_REGIONS → N`) so concurrent per-task VMs are possible. Cheapest unblock; still per-task spin/kill + N× idle risk if left running.
- **One persistent GPU host + a job QUEUE.** The decoupled worker already pulls work (`claim_due_job` / `python -m backend.worker {infer|train}`) — a single longer-lived GPU box draining a shared queue would let multiple sessions submit without each provisioning. Trade: idle GPU bills when no jobs (~$0.35–0.70/hr) → needs an auto-stop-when-idle guard.
- **Cloud Run + GPU (scale-to-zero).** Per-request GPU, no idle cost — the strong fit for per-video billing economics (engine DECOUPLED_COMPUTE #246; appliance `LOCAL_APPLIANCE_ARCHITECTURE.md` scale path). Needs the `backend.worker` CONTAINERIZED (WASB's torch-1.11 conda env makes the image non-trivial — flagged as a likely gap).

**How to apply:** treat as an infra/compute-track design item alongside the appliance/decoupled arch + per-video cost ceiling (appliance §10). When the owner wants to act, lead with the queue-on-one-host vs Cloud-Run-scale-to-zero trade and the idle-cost guard. === gpu_setup.sh: key paths + conda env ===
3:# Native (no-WSL) WASB shuttle-detector setup for a Linux GPU box — the M3.5 enabler for
4:# real trajectory extraction. Replicates docs/TRACKNET_WSL_SETUP.md's WSL conda env
6:# main venv); this builds a SEPARATE conda env for WASB (torch 1.11) — never co-installed
11:# ⚠ WASB-C3: the pretrained badminton weights' license / source-dataset provenance is NOT
16:MODELS_DIR="${RALLY_MODELS_DIR:-$HOME/models}"
17:CONDA_DIR="${CONDA_DIR:-$HOME/miniconda3}"
18:WASB_REPO="$MODELS_DIR/WASB-SBDT"
19:mkdir -p "$MODELS_DIR"
21:echo "[gpu] [1/5] miniconda (native Linux)"
22:if [ ! -x "$CONDA_DIR/bin/conda" ]; then
23:  curl -sSL https://repo.anaconda.com/miniconda/Miniconda3-latest-Linux-x86_64.sh -o /tmp/mc.sh
27:source "$CONDA_DIR/etc/profile.d/conda.sh"
29:# Modern conda BLOCKS non-interactive `conda create` until the defaults-channel Terms of Service
32:conda tos accept --override-channels --channel https://repo.anaconda.com/pkgs/main >/dev/null 2>&1 || true
33:conda tos accept --override-channels --channel https://repo.anaconda.com/pkgs/r    >/dev/null 2>&1 || true
35:echo "[gpu] [2/5] WASB-SBDT repo"
36:if [ ! -d "$WASB_REPO/.git" ]; then
37:  git clone --depth 1 https://github.com/nttcom/WASB-SBDT.git "$WASB_REPO"
40:echo "[gpu] [3/5] conda env 'wasb' (py3.8 + torch 1.11.0+cu113 — the verified WASB stack)"
41:conda env list | grep -q '/envs/wasb$' || conda create -y -q -n wasb python=3.8
42:conda activate wasb
45:[ -f "$WASB_REPO/requirements.txt" ] && python -m pip install -q -r "$WASB_REPO/requirements.txt" || true
46:# WASB-SBDT's requirements.txt is INCOMPLETE: src/ imports hydra, pandas, etc. that it omits, so
51:echo "[gpu] [4/5] pretrained badminton weights"
52:WEIGHTS="$WASB_REPO/pretrained_weights/wasb_badminton_best.pth.tar"
68:echo "[gpu] native WASB env ready.  Activate with:  source $CONDA_DIR/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate wasb"

### 698. assistant (2026-06-21T01:07:58.865Z)

**Confirmed root cause of "no logs":** `backend/worker/__main__.py` never configures logging (no `basicConfig`), so every `logger.info`/`logger.error` from the segmenter and native runner — including "Running native WASB inference…", the detector choice, and availability failures — is silently suppressed at the default WARNING level. `backend.main` configures it; the worker doesn't. **That's why the cold-start gave zero diagnostics.**

Let me read the worker's `main()` + argparse to add logging config + a 0-result diagnostic:

1	"""Worker dispatcher — pull from storage → run the pipeline → push outputs back.
2	
3	    python -m backend.worker infer --video-uri <uri> --out-uri <prefix> \

5	    python -m backend.worker train ...        # Gen-0 train-from-bucket (follow-up)
6	
7	The URI scheme selects the storage backend via ``backend.storage`` (a bare path / ``local://``
8	is an identity passthrough; ``s3://`` / ``gcs://`` is an object store), so existing local runs
9	are byte-for-byte unchanged. Combine with ``RALLY_DETECTOR_IMPL=stub`` and

### 753. assistant (2026-06-21T01:22:23.698Z)

All three asks handled. Here's the close-out.

## 1. Fix PR — merged ✅
The cold-start surfaced one concrete, fixable bug, and I merged it: **[engine #271](https://github.com/Khelsutra/badminton-highlight-indexer/pull/271)** (full gate green — 1027 tests, ruff + mypy clean; built in an isolated worktree so the concurrent session's checkout was untouched). No other PRs are open in either repo.

## 2. Why the 0-rally run was bad — and the real failure
You're right that 0 rallies is wrong (the smoke clip gives 22). But the **deeper problem was that we couldn't see *why*** — the run produced no diagnostics at all. I traced that to the actual root cause:

> **`backend/worker/__main__.py` never configured logging.** The pipeline *already* logs richly — which detector it picked, "Running native WASB inference…", and availability failures — but the worker ran at the default WARNING level with no handler, so **every one of those lines was silently dropped.** `backend.main` sets up logging; the worker didn't.

So the 0-rally cause itself (my minimal config didn't match the engine session's *tuned* native-WASB invocation, and full-res `mahadevpura-1` ≠ the proxy resolution) would have been visible in the logs — if there had been any.

## 3. Logs for next time ✅ (the main ask)
The fix makes a 0-rally run **fully analysable from the log**:
- **The worker now configures logging** at the CLI entry (INFO; `force=True` so it reliably owns the level — a test caught that `basicConfig` is a no-op when handlers already exist) + a **`-v/--verbose`** flag for DEBUG (the native detector command, frame counts).
- **`worker infer` now emits a loud, explanatory WARNING on a 0-segment success** — `video_id`, the resolved segmenter + `detector_impl`, and the likely causes (native runner unavailable / WASB produced no detections / input resolution ≠ tuned proxy res), with "re-run with `-v`".

Next cold start, instead of a silent "0 segment(s)", we'll get the detector's own logs **plus** a one-line diagnosis of what went wrong.

I also restored the smoke clip's `intervals.csv`/`report.json` that the diagnostic run had clobbered (back to 22 segments) and cleaned the junk outputs. The one remaining piece for a cold-start that actually produces rallies is the engine session's exact tuned `worker infer` config/command — and now when we run it, the logs will tell us immediately if anything's off.

## What changed
The Request-Access form now gives **immediate, obvious feedback** on submit. The moment a valid form is sent:
- the button swaps to a **spinning loader + "Sending…"**,
- goes **disabled** (with `cursor: progress`),
- and sets **`aria-busy`** for screen readers.

On failure it's **restored** before the email fallback, so you're never stuck on a frozen "Sending…".

### 823. assistant (2026-06-21T02:41:35.195Z)

Done — verified, fixed, and live on both hosts (parity in sync; `reel-close` present on Cloudflare and the droplet).

## Your question, answered
**No — clicking or playing one video does not hide the other.** I triggered the post-signup reveal and inspected both: the tool screencast (680×426) and the reel (678×381) were both `visible`, and they **stayed visible** before *and* after I clicked + played the tool video. There's no hide-on-interaction logic.

## But I found the real cause of "they can't see it"
The single **30-second countdown was closing *both* videos together.** The tool demo alone is ~23s, so someone who watched it and then wanted the reel could lose everything before they got to it — which feels like "I touched one and the other vanished." (That's also what tripped me up mid-test: an eval came back with the video "gone" simply because 30s of wall-clock had elapsed.)

## Fix (shipped — [khelsutra#37](https://github.com/avidullu/khelsutra/pull/37))
- The auto-close now **holds while the reel is playing** (`onStateChange` PLAYING → pause the timer), so a viewer is **never cut off mid-watch**. It still closes as a privacy safety-net when the reel isn't playing.
- Added a manual **"✕ Close"** so the user dismisses on demand — verified it closes the reveal cleanly.
- Clearer copy: *"auto-closes Ns after the reel stops — or close it anytime."*
- Both videos remain visible together throughout.

Site suite **36 green** (new test guards the manual close + the hold-while-playing), merged, droplet re-synced, Cloudflare deployed (~24s), **parity in sync**.

### 828. user (2026-06-21T02:44:17.647Z)

=== VIDEO_RECORDING_GUIDELINES.md (engine, from master) ===
# Video Recording Guidelines (DRAFT — thresholds pending empirical data)

**Status:** 🚧 Draft / placeholder · **Date:** 2026-05-30
**Why this exists:** highlight quality is bounded by *input* quality. To get reliably good auto-generated highlights, the source video must be recorded a certain way (camera height, court position, resolution, frame rate, stability, framing). We want to (1) **derive** the minimum acceptable capture spec **empirically** from the precision/recall of the solutions we build, and (2) **publish user-facing best practices** so a user knows how to record footage that the product can index well.

> **This is a tracked thought, not a finished standard.** The exact numeric thresholds below are **placeholders** to be replaced with values measured against our golden set. See "How we'll set the real numbers" (§3). Captured now (per the team's working agreement) so it isn't lost; to be revisited.
>
> **Update (2026-06-02):** the **frame-rate** and **camera-geometry** rows now carry **literature-backed starting values** (cited inline). They remain *starting points* to confirm empirically per §3. The golden-set **corpus** diversity spec (what footage to collect, not just how users should record) is now in [GOLDEN_SET_PHASE1_VIDEOS.md](GOLDEN_SET_PHASE1_VIDEOS.md).
>
> **Update (2026-06-07):** **AUDIO is now a first-class capture requirement** (new row below), per ADR-007 — we will use the shuttle "thwack" as a rally cue (audio+visual fusion lifts recall). This is a hard "record with audio on, mic unobstructed" ask, not a placeholder; efficacy on real GoPro audio is being validated (experiment E1 in [AUDIO_RALLY_DETECTION_PLAN.md](archives/research/AUDIO_RALLY_DETECTION_PLAN.md)).

---

## ⚠️ THE #1 CAPTURE RULE: ONE MATCH PER FRAME (single court)

> **For the best auto-highlights, record a SINGLE match on a SINGLE court — with no other matches
> playing in the background or on adjacent courts.** This is the happy path the product is tuned for,
> and as of **2026-06-18 it is MEASURED, not a guess:**
>
> - On a golden **multi-court** clip, the rally detector fired **87 "rallies" against 43 real ones —
>   precision ≈ 0.20 (~80% false positives)**, almost all of them background-court games it mistook
>   for play.
> - On **single-court** clips, precision is markedly higher and rally boundaries are tight; local even
>   **out-recalls Gemini** on normal single-match play.
>
> **Multi-court is a KNOWN BOTTLENECK we are actively working to fix** (player-state + court-occupancy
> signals — see [RALLY_DETECTION_GAP_CLOSURE.md](RALLY_DETECTION_GAP_CLOSURE.md)). Until that lands,
> **single-match capture is strongly recommended** — a reel made from busy multi-court footage *will*
> include false "rallies" from the neighbouring games. **North star (for now): consistently beat Gemini
> on the single-match happy path.**

---

## 1. Why input capture matters (grounded in what we already know)

ADR-004 found a concrete, capture-dependent failure mode: on **broadcast** footage the camera *tracks the players*, so player pixel-motion dips during rallies and spikes on pans/replays — making player-motion a poor rally proxy. The lesson generalizes: **the pipeline's accuracy is conditional on how the video was shot.** A **static, full-court** camera (typical of a fixed tripod recording) behaves very differently from broadcast — and is likely much friendlier to both the motion/detector stages and the shuttle-tracking substrate (WASB/TrackNet, ADR-001).

So capture guidance isn't cosmetic — it directly moves precision/recall.

---

## 2. The dimensions that likely matter (hypotheses to test)

| Dimension | Why it plausibly affects P/R | Placeholder guidance (UNVERIFIED) |
|---|---|---|
| **Resolution** | Shuttle is tiny & fast; too few pixels → detector misses it | ≥ **1080p** preferred; **720p** likely floor; 2K/4K may help shuttle tracking but ↑ compute/cost |
| **Frame rate** | Motion blur; boundary precision; fast-ball detection | ≥ **30 fps** badminton/pickleball; **≥ 60 fps table tennis** — 30 fps collapses fast-ball detection (**22.5% → 93.2%** at 60 fps; 120 ≈ 60, [Nature Sci Rep](https://www.nature.com/articles/s41598-023-42966-6)) |
| **Camera position** | Static full-court view avoids the broadcast camera-tracking trap (ADR-004) | **Fixed tripod**, elevated, behind/above one baseline, whole court in frame |
| **Camera height & angle** | Occlusion of players/shuttle; court keystoning | **3–5 m** elevated (~4.5 m), **~30° down-tilt**, behind one baseline, whole court + net headroom ([badminton-CV setup](https://pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/articles/PMC10747833/)) |
| **Framing / margins** | Cropping the court or players breaks detection & relevance check | Entire court + a margin; players never leave frame |
| **Stability** | Handheld jitter inflates motion signals | Tripod / fixed mount; no panning or zoom during play |
| **Lighting / exposure** | Blur & contrast affect detection and the existing blur/relevance validators | Even indoor lighting; avoid backlight/glare; minimize motion blur |
| **Single match in frame (no background games) — ✅ MEASURED, the #1 rule** | Adjacent/background-court games get detected as false rallies — this is the **dominant precision failure** | **One match per frame, single court.** Multi-court precision measured at **≈0.20** (vs ≈0.31 single, tighter boundaries); multi-court fix in progress — see [RALLY_DETECTION_GAP_CLOSURE.md](RALLY_DETECTION_GAP_CLOSURE.md) |
| **Court contrast / background** | Relevance check uses court color; busy crowds add false motion | Clear court markings; minimal moving background where possible |
| **Codec / container** | Pipeline already validates MP4 + decodes H.264/HEVC | MP4 (H.264/HEVC) as today; align with `archives/MONETIZATION_AUDIT.md` codec notes |
| **Audio (REQUIRED — ADR-007)** | Shuttle "thwack" hits are a strong rally cue; audio+visual fusion lifts rally *recall* (the WASB bottleneck) — see ADR-007 / archives/research/AUDIO_RALLY_DETECTION_PLAN.md | **Record with AUDIO ENABLED** (GoPro default — do not mute). Keep the **mic unobstructed**; **avoid aggressive wind/noise reduction** that flattens the sharp impact transient. Camera reasonably close to the court helps SNR (shuttle hits are quiet). |

These map onto checks that **already exist** in `backend/pipeline/validators/` (resolution, fps, blur, scene-cut, court relevance) — so several can be enforced/measured programmatically rather than only advised.

---

## 3. How we'll set the REAL numbers (empirical plan)

Replace every placeholder above with a value derived from data, using the golden-set harness:

1. Take golden-set clips (GOLDEN_SET_VENDORING.md) spanning a **range** of each dimension — e.g. the same match re-encoded at 480p/720p/1080p/4K, or clips at different camera heights.
2. Run the production pipeline; score with `backend/eval/metrics.py`.
3. Plot **precision/recall vs. each dimension** → find the knee where quality collapses.
4. Set the **published minimum** at (knee + safety margin); set a **"recommended"** tier above it.
5. Re-derive when the pipeline changes materially (new detector/segmenter shifts the knees).

This turns "record in 1080p" from an assertion into a measured threshold — and lets us tell users *why*.

---

## 4. Two surfaces for the output

1. **User-facing best-practices guide** — short, friendly, illustrated: "Put the camera here, this high, record at ≥X, keep it still." The goal is that a user can record once and get great results.
2. **Programmatic pre-flight check** — extend `backend/pipeline/validators/` to *reject or warn* on out-of-spec uploads at ingest (resolution/fps/blur/framing), so the product fails fast with actionable feedback instead of producing poor highlights silently.

---

## 5. Open questions
1. Which dimension dominates — resolution, camera staticness, or height? (Run the ablations in §3 to rank them.)
2. Do we **enforce** minimums (reject uploads) or only **warn**? Likely warn first, enforce once thresholds are trustworthy.
3. Single fixed-camera spec, or several supported "recording profiles" (e.g. baseline-elevated vs. side-elevated)?
4. How does the owned/licensed golden corpus need to grow to *cover* the capture conditions real users will actually use? (Feeds GOLDEN_SET_VENDORING.md §8.)

54	</head>
55	<body>
56	<div class="sheet">
57	  <div class="head">
58	    <span class="brand">Khel<span class="play" aria-hidden="true"></span>Sutra</span>
59	    <h1>1-page capture checklist</h1>
60	  </div>
61	  <div class="body">
62	    <p class="intro">Great highlights start with a steady, full-court recording. Five minutes of setup makes a big difference to your reels.</p>
63	
64	    <div class="rule">
65	      <span class="star" aria-hidden="true">★</span>
66	      <div>
67	        <b>The golden rule: one match, one court, in full frame — and don't move the camera.</b>
68	        <p>Multi-court footage is the hard case we're still sharpening; a single court in frame gives the best results today.</p>
69	      </div>
70	    </div>
71	
72	    <div class="grid">
73	      <div class="step">
74	        <h3><span class="n">1</span>Place the camera</h3>
75	        <ul>
76	          <li>Behind <b>one baseline</b>, elevated — a high tripod, a few metres up, or a balcony.</li>
77	          <li>Tilt down about <b>30°</b>; keep the <b>whole court</b> in frame with a little margin and net headroom.</li>
78	          <li>Players should never leave the frame.</li>
79	        </ul>
80	      </div>
81	      <div class="step">
82	        <h3><span class="n">2</span>Settings</h3>
83	        <ul>
84	          <li><b>1080p or higher</b>, shot in <b>landscape</b>.</li>
85	          <li><b>30 fps</b> for badminton/pickleball; <b>60 fps</b> for table tennis.</li>
86	          <li><b>Audio ON</b> (don't mute) — keep the mic unobstructed, wind/noise reduction off.</li>
87	        </ul>
88	      </div>
89	      <div class="step">
90	        <h3><span class="n">3</span>Keep it still</h3>
91	        <ul>
92	          <li>Fixed tripod or mount.</li>
93	          <li><b>No panning, no zooming</b> during play.</li>
94	        </ul>
95	      </div>
96	      <div class="step">
97	        <h3><span class="n">4</span>Light &amp; record</h3>
98	        <ul>
99	          <li>Even indoor lighting; avoid backlight, glare and very dark halls.</li>
100	          <li>Record the <b>whole session</b> and upload it as-is — KhelSutra does the cutting.</li>
101	        </ul>
102	      </div>
103	    </div>
104	
105	    <div class="cols">
106	      <div class="do">
107	        <h4>Do</h4>
108	        <ul>
109	          <li>Fixed camera, full court, behind the baseline</li>
110	          <li>1080p+, landscape, audio on</li>
111	          <li>One match on one court</li>
112	        </ul>
113	      </div>
114	      <div class="dont">
115	        <h4>Avoid</h4>
116	        <ul>
117	          <li>Handheld or phone-in-hand</li>
118	          <li>Broadcast-style pans &amp; zooming</li>
119	          <li>Several courts in one frame; very dark halls</li>
120	        </ul>
121	      </div>
122	    </div>
123	
124	    <p class="consent">Please record adults with their okay. Footage involving minors is never used to improve the tool and is never published.</p>
125	  </div>
126	  <div class="foot">
127	    <span>Questions? <a href="mailto:hello@khelsutra.guru">hello@khelsutra.guru</a> · <a href="https://khelsutra.guru">khelsutra.guru</a></span>
128	    <span class="noprint">Tip: press Ctrl/Cmd + P to print or save as PDF.</span>
129	  </div>
130	</div>
131	</body>
132	</html>
